## Mackenzie Valley Resource Management Act {#mackenzie-valley-resource-management-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1998, c. 25
:::

Assented to 1998-06-18

An Act to provide for an integrated system of land and water management
in the Mackenzie Valley, to establish certain boards for that purpose
and to make consequential amendments to other Acts
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
###### Preamble {#preamble .MarginalNote}

WHEREAS the Gwich'in Comprehensive Land Claim Agreement and the Sahtu
Dene and Metis Comprehensive Land Claim Agreement require the
establishment of land use planning boards and land and water boards for
the settlement areas referred to in those Agreements and the
establishment of an environmental impact review board for the Mackenzie
Valley, and provide as well for the establishment of a land and water
board for an area extending beyond those settlement areas;

WHEREAS the Agreements require that those boards be established as
institutions of public government within an integrated and coordinated
system of land and water management in the Mackenzie Valley;

AND WHEREAS the intent of the Agreements as acknowledged by the parties
is to establish those boards for the purpose of regulating all land and
water uses, including deposits of waste, in the settlement areas for
which they are established or in the Mackenzie Valley, as the case may
be;
:::

::: Enacts
NOW, THEREFORE, Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the
Senate and House of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-345757 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Mackenzie Valley Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-345760 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Act.

[[Déline Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Déline Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Déline Final
    Self-Government Agreement among the Déline First Nation Band, the
    Déline Land Corporation, the Government of Canada and the Government
    of the Northwest Territories, signed on February 18, 2015, including
    any amendments made to it, that is approved, given effect and
    declared valid by the [Déline Final Self-Government Agreement
    Act](/eng/acts/D-1.5). ([accord de Deline]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Déline Got'ine Government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Déline Got'ine Government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    government established in accordance with chapter 3 of the Déline
    Agreement. ([gouvernement Gotine de Deline]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Déline lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Déline lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the lands whose title is
    vested in the Déline Got'ine Government in accordance with 21.2.1 of
    the Déline Agreement. ([terres de Deline]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Déline law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Déline law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
    definition [[DGG Law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in chapter 1 of the
    Déline Agreement. ([loi de Deline]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[deposit of waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[deposit of waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a deposit of waste in
    any waters in the Mackenzie Valley or in any other place under
    conditions in which the waste, or any other waste that results from
    the deposit of that waste, may enter any waters in the Mackenzie
    Valley. ([dépôt de déchets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the components of the
    Earth and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} land, water and air, including all layers of the
      atmosphere;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all organic and inorganic matter and living
      organisms; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the interacting natural systems that include
      components referred to in paragraphs (a) and
      (b). ([environnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[federal Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Northern Affairs. ([ministre fédéral]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[first nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[first nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in First
    Nation, the Sahtu First Nation or bodies representing other Dene or
    Metis of the North Slave, South Slave or Deh Cho region of the
    Mackenzie Valley, but does not include the Tlicho First Nation or
    the Tlicho Government. ([première nation]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Gwich'in Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Gwich'in Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Comprehensive
    Land Claim Agreement between Her Majesty the Queen in right of
    Canada and the Gwich'in as represented by the Gwich'in Tribal
    Council, signed on April 22, 1992 and approved, given effect and
    declared valid by the [Gwich'in Land Claim Settlement
    Act](/eng/acts/G-11.8), as that Agreement is amended from time to
    time in accordance with its provisions. ([accord
    gwich'in]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Gwich'in First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Gwich'in First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in as
    represented by the Gwich'in Tribal Council referred to in the
    Gwich'in Agreement or by any successor to it. ([première nation des
    Gwich'in]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[harvesting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[harvesting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to wildlife, means
    hunting, trapping or fishing activities carried on in conformity
    with a land claim agreement or, in respect of persons and places not
    subject to a land claim agreement, carried on pursuant to aboriginal
    or treaty rights. ([exploitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[heritage resources]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[heritage resources]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means archaeological or
    historic sites, burial sites, artifacts and other objects of
    historical, cultural or religious significance, and historical or
    cultural records. ([ressources patrimoniales]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[land claim agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[land claim agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in
    Agreement, the Sahtu Agreement or the Tlicho Agreement. ([accord de
    revendication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[local government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[local government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any local government
    established under the laws of the Northwest Territories, including a
    city, town, village, hamlet, charter community, settlement or
    government of a Tlicho community, whether incorporated or not, and
    includes the territorial government in the case where it is acting
    in the place of that local government in accordance with those laws.
    It also includes the Déline Got'ine Government in the case where it
    is exercising the jurisdiction and authority set out in 9.1 of the
    Déline Agreement. ([administration locale]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Mackenzie Valley]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Mackenzie Valley]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that part of the
    Northwest Territories bounded on the south by the 60th parallel of
    latitude, on the west by Yukon, on the north by the Inuvialuit
    Settlement Region, as defined in the Agreement given effect by the
    [Western Arctic (Inuvialuit) Claims Settlement Act](/eng/acts/W-6.7)
    , and on the east by the Nunavut Settlement Area, as defined in the
    [Nunavut Land Claims Agreement Act](/eng/acts/N-28.7) , but does not
    include Wood Buffalo National Park of Canada. ([vallée du
    Mackenzie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Monfwi Gogha De Niitlee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Monfwi Gogha De Niitlee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area
    described in part 1 of the appendix to chapter 1 of the Tlicho
    Agreement. ([Monfwi gogha de niitlee]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Sahtu Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Sahtu Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Comprehensive Land
    Claim Agreement between Her Majesty the Queen in right of Canada and
    the Sahtu Dene and Metis as represented by the Sahtu Tribal Council,
    signed on September 6, 1993 and approved, given effect and declared
    valid by the [Sahtu Dene and Metis Land Claim Settlement
    Act](/eng/acts/S-1.5), as that Agreement is amended from time to
    time in accordance with its provisions. ([accord du
    Sahtu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Sahtu First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Sahtu First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Sahtu Dene and
    Metis as represented by The Sahtu Secretariat Incorporated, a
    corporation without share capital under Part II of the [Canada
    Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-1.8), R.S.C. 1970, c. C-32, being the
    successor, for the purposes of this Act, to the Sahtu Tribal Council
    referred to in the Sahtu Agreement, or by any successor to that
    corporation. It also includes the Déline Got'ine Government in the
    case where The Sahtu Secretariat Incorporated or its successor has
    made a delegation or assignment to that government of any powers and
    functions conferred under this Act. ([première nation du
    Sahtu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[settlement area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[settlement area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area described in
    appendix A to the Gwich'in Agreement or in appendix A to the Sahtu
    Agreement. ([région désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[settlement lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[settlement lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means lands referred to as
    settlement lands in the Gwich'in Agreement or the Sahtu
    Agreement. ([terres désignées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[territorial government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[territorial government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the government
    of the Northwest Territories. ([gouvernement
    territorial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[territorial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[territorial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any law of the
    Legislature of the Northwest Territories. ([règle de droit
    territoriale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[territorial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[territorial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any
    provision of this Act, means the minister of the territorial
    government designated by instrument of the Executive Council of the
    Northwest Territories for the purposes of that provision. ([ministre
    territorial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Land Claims and
    Self-Government Agreement among the Tlicho, the Government of the
    Northwest Territories and the Government of Canada, signed on August
    25, 2003 and approved, given effect and declared valid by the
    [Tlicho Land Claims and Self-Government Act](/eng/acts/T-11.3), as
    that Agreement is amended from time to time in accordance with its
    provisions. ([accord tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho citizen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho citizen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person whose name is
    on the Register as defined in chapter 1 of the Tlicho
    Agreement. ([citoyen tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho community]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho community]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a community for which
    a community government is established in accordance with chapter 8
    of the Tlicho Agreement. ([collectivité tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho First Nation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the aboriginal
    people of Canada to whom section 35 of the [Constitution Act,
    1982](/eng/Const/) applies, consisting of all Tlicho
    citizens. ([première nation tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho Government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho Government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the government of
    the Tlicho First Nation established in accord­ance with chapter 7 of
    the Tlicho Agreement. ([gouvernement tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Tlicho lands as defined
    in chapter 1 of the Tlicho Agreement. ([terres
    tlichos]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Tlicho law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Tlicho law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a law enacted by the Tlicho
    Government. ([loi tlicho]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Wekeezhii]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Wekeezhii]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area described in part 2
    of the appendix to chapter 1 of the Tlicho
    Agreement. ([Wekeezhii]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** Wherever in this Act
reference is made, in relation to any matter, to a power or duty to
consult, that power or duty shall be exercised

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by providing, to the party to be consulted,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} notice of the matter in sufficient form and detail
    to allow the party to prepare its views on the matter,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a reasonable period for the party to prepare those
    views, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an opportunity to present those views to the
    party having the power or duty to consult; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} by considering, fully and impartially, any views so
  presented.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister may delegate, in writing, any of his or her powers,
  duties or functions under this Act --- either generally or as
  otherwise provided in the instrument of delegation --- to the minister
  of the Northwest Territories designated by the Commissioner of the
  Northwest Territories for the purposes of this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Included functions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The functions of the federal Minister referred to in
  subsection (1) include the power to delegate duties pursuant to
  section 122.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation to aboriginal organizations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A first nation may, in conformity with its land claim
  agreement, delegate any of the functions of the first nation under
  this Act to an aboriginal organization designated by it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation by Tlicho Government

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government may, in conformity with the
  Tlicho Agreement, delegate any of its functions under this Act to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body or office established by a Tlicho law;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any department, agency or office of the federal or
    the territorial government;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a board or other public body established by or
    under an Act of Parliament or by a territorial law; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a local government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation by Déline Got'ine Government

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Déline Got'ine Government may, in conformity with
  the Déline Agreement, delegate any of its functions under this Act to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body or office established by a Déline law;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a department, agency or office of the federal or
    the territorial government;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a board or other public body established by or
    under an Act of Parliament or by a territorial law; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 4
- 2005, c. 1, s. 16
- 2014, c. 2, s. 114
- 2015, c. 24, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If there
  is any inconsistency or conflict between this Act and a land claim
  agreement, the Déline Agreement, an Act giving effect to any of those
  agreements or the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5), then the land claim
  agreement, the Déline Agreement, the Act or the [Indian
  Act](/eng/acts/I-5) prevails over this Act to the extent of the
  inconsistency or conflict.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of Indigenous peoples

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Act is to be construed as upholding the
  Aboriginal and treaty rights of Indigenous peoples recognized and
  affirmed by section 35 of the [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/),
  and not as abrogating or derogating from them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indigenous peoples

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), [[Indigenous
  peoples]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
  definition [[aboriginal peoples of Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  subsection 35(2) of the [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 5
- 2015, c. 24, s. 24
- [2024, c. 30, s. 17](#1496446-1497735){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1496446-1497735.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20160901.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement between Tlicho Government and
another aboriginal people

**[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1 .sectionLabel}** The rights of the
Tlicho First Nation, Tlicho citizens and the Tlicho Government under
this Act are subject to the provisions of any agreement entered into
between the Tlicho Government and an aboriginal people, other than the
Tlicho First Nation, under 2.7.3 of chapter 2 of the Tlicho Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 17
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limits

- **[[5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  failure of any of the following to exercise a power or perform a duty
  or function within a period or time limit fixed or prescribed under
  this Act does not terminate their authority to do so nor does it
  invalidate any document prepared or submitted or any decision or
  action taken in the exercise of such a power or the performance of
  such duty or function:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the federal Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Gwich'in Land Use Planning Board;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Sahtu Land Use Planning Board;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Gwich'in Land and Water Board;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Sahtu Land and Water Board;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a responsible minister, as defined in section 111;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Mackenzie Valley Environmental Impact Review
    Board or one of its panels;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a joint panel established under subsection 140(2)
    or paragraph 141(2)(b) or (3)(a) or a review panel referred to in
    subsection 40(2) of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75);
    and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a designated regulatory agency, as defined in
    section 111.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the periods fixed by regulations referred to in
    subsections 138.1(2), (3) and (4) and 141(4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other period or time limit fixed by regulations
    that is exempted from the application of subsection (1) by
    regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by regulation, exempt a
  period or time limit set out in the regulations, other than those
  referred to in paragraph 2(a), from the application of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 115
- [2019, c. 28, s. 164](#1158308-1199234){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1158308-1199234.html"
  role="button"}

## [Application and Consultation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-345860 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Except where otherwise
provided, this Act applies in the Mackenzie Valley.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to Her Majesty

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on Her
Majesty in right of Canada or a province, except that Her Majesty in
right of Canada is not required to pay any fee prescribed by regulations
made under paragraph 90.3(1)(k) or subparagraph 90.3(2)(a)(i).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 7
- 2014, c. 2, s. 116
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waters vested in Her Majesty

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** Subject to any rights,
powers or privileges granted or preserved under the [Dominion Water
Power Act](/eng/acts/W-4), the property in and the right to the use and
flow of all waters are vested in Her Majesty in right of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 116
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other Acts, etc., to be complied with

**[[7.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.2 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
nothing in this Act, the regulations or a licence or permit, as defined
in section 51, authorizes a person to contravene or fail to comply with
any other Act or any regulation or order made under it, except as
provided in that other Act, regulation or order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 116
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister shall consult the first nations, the Tlicho
  Government and the Déline Got'ine Government with respect to the
  amendment of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall, in the course of any
  negotiations with a first nation relating to self-government, review
  the pertinent provisions of this Act in consultation with that first
  nation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 8
- 2005, c. 1, s. 18
- 2015, c. 24, s. 25
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions Respecting Boards]{.HTitleText1} {#h-345882 .Part}

### [Establishment and Organization]{.HTitleText2} {#h-345883 .Subheading}

Definition of [[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any board established by this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.1 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of the
establishment of boards by this Act is to enable residents of the
Mackenzie Valley to participate in the management of its resources for
the benefit of the residents and of other Canadians.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Capacity

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** A board has, for the
purposes of its functions, the capacity, rights, powers and privileges
of a natural person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of members by federal
Minister

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  members of a board --- other than the chairperson, any members
  appointed pursuant to a determination under section 15 and any members
  appointed by the Tlicho Government under subsection 57.1(2) or in
  accordance with an agreement referred to in that subsection --- shall
  be appointed by the federal Minister in accordance with Parts 2 to 5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of the Wekeezhii Land and Water
  Board, the federal Minister may appoint

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} alternate members selected from persons nominated
    for that purpose by a first nation, or selected following
    consultation with first nations, to act in the event of the absence
    or incapacity of members appointed on such nomination or following
    such consultation, respectively; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} alternate members agreed to by the territorial
    Minister to act in the event of the absence or incapacity of members
    other than members referred to in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 11
- 2005, c. 1, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Chairperson

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  in the case of the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board, the chairperson of
  a board shall be appointed by the federal Minister from persons
  nominated by a majority of the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment by federal Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of the Wekeezhii Land and Water
  Board, if a majority of the members does not nominate a person
  acceptable to the federal Minister within a reasonable time, the
  Minister may appoint any person as chairperson of the board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wekeezhii Land and Water Board

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The chairperson of the Wekeezhii Land and Water
  Board shall be appointed jointly by the federal Minister and the
  Tlicho Government on the nomination of the members of the Board other
  than the chairperson.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence or incapacity of chairperson

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The board may designate a member to act as its
  chairperson during the absence or incapacity of the chairperson or a
  vacancy in the office of chairperson, and that person while so acting
  may exercise the powers and shall perform the duties of the
  chairperson.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 12
- 2005, c. 1, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of chairperson

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** The chairperson of a
board is its chief executive officer and has the powers and duties
prescribed by the by-laws of the board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of office

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member of a board holds office for a term of three years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reappointment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member may be reappointed in the same or another
  capacity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal by federal Minister after
  consultation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A member who has been appointed by the federal
  Minister may not be removed from office except after consultation by
  the federal Minister with the board and, where applicable, with the
  territorial Minister, the first nation or the Tlicho Government that
  nominated the member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal by Tlicho Government after
  consultation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A member of the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board who
  has been appointed by the Tlicho Government may not be removed from
  office except after consultation by the Tlicho Government with the
  Board and the federal Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 14
- 2005, c. 1, s. 21
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation of right of representation
of other Aboriginal peoples

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** Despite any provision of
this Act respecting members of a board, if an Aboriginal people has a
right under a land claim agreement to representation on that board in
relation to a decision of the board that might affect an area used by
that Aboriginal people that is outside the board's area of jurisdiction,
the board shall, in accordance with that land claim agreement, determine
how to implement that right, so long as the number of members who are
either nominated by a first nation, nominated or appointed by the Tlicho
Government or appointed following consultation by the federal Minister
with the first nations of the regions of the Mackenzie Valley outside
the settlement areas and Wekeezhii, as the case may be, and who are
temporarily appointed to implement that right remains equal to the
number of other members not including the chairperson.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 15
- 2005, c. 1, s. 22
- [2019, c. 19, s. 2](#1151716-1173325){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151716-1173325.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member of a board may not act in relation to an application to the
  board or participate in a decision of the board that would place the
  member in a material conflict of interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status or entitlements under agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member of a board is not placed in a material
  conflict of interest merely because of any status or entitlement
  conferred on the member under the Gwich'in Agreement, the Sahtu
  Agreement, the Tlicho Agreement or any other agreement between a first
  nation and Her Majesty in right of Canada for the settlement of a
  claim to lands or under the Déline Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 16
- 2005, c. 1, s. 23
- 2015, c. 24, s. 26
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Members of a board, including any
  members appointed in accordance with a determination under section 15,
  shall be paid the fees or other remuneration that the federal Minister
  may fix.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenses

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Such members shall be paid such travel and living
  expenses, incurred by them while absent from their ordinary place of
  residence in the course of performing their duties, as are consistent
  with directives of the Treasury Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 17
- 2005, c. 1, s. 24
- 2014, c. 2, s. 123
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staff

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board may employ such persons and engage the services of such agents,
  advisers and experts as are necessary for the proper conduct of its
  business and may fix the conditions of their employment or engagement
  and pay their remuneration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sharing of staff and facilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The boards may share staff and facilities with one
  another for the effective and efficient conduct of their affairs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** The members of a board
and its employees are deemed to be employees for the purposes of the
[Government Employees Compensation Act](/eng/acts/G-5) and to be
employed in the federal public administration for the purposes of any
regulations made pursuant to section 9 of the [Aeronautics
Act](/eng/acts/A-2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 19
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection from personal liability

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** The members and employees
of a board are not liable for anything done or omitted to be done in
good faith in the exercise or performance or purported exercise or
performance of any powers, duties or functions under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 20
- 2014, c. 2, s. 124
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General Powers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-345954 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision by majority

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  decision of a majority of the members of a board present at a meeting
  is a decision of the board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation by telephone

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the by-laws of a board, any member may
  participate in a meeting by means of telephone or other communications
  facilities that are likely to enable all persons participating in the
  meeting to hear each other, and a member so participating is deemed to
  be present at the meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government information

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Subject to any other
federal or territorial law and to any Tlicho law or Déline law, a board
may obtain from any department or agency of the federal or territorial
government, the Tlicho Government or the Déline Got'ine Government any
information in the possession of the department, agency, Tlicho
Government or Déline Got'ine Government that the board requires for the
performance of its functions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 22
- 2005, c. 1, s. 25
- 2015, c. 24, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of orders

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** A decision or order of a
board may be made an order of the Supreme Court of the Northwest
Territories by the filing of a certified copy of it with the registrar
of the Court, and a decision or order so filed is enforceable in the
same manner as an order of that Court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearings

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** In addition to hearings
that a board is authorized or required to hold under this Act, a board
may conduct any hearings that it considers to be desirable for the
purpose of carrying out any of its functions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 24
- 2005, c. 1, s. 26
- 2014, c. 2, s. 125(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordination

**[[24.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.1 .sectionLabel}** A board shall
coordinate its activities, including hearings, with the activities of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} other boards;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} departments and agencies of the federal government
  that have responsibility for the administration, management and
  control of parks to which the [Canada National Parks
  Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies or lands acquired pursuant to the
  [Historic Sites and Monuments Act](/eng/acts/H-4);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} committees established under any of the land claim
  agreements for the management of parks to which the [Canada National
  Parks Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} committees, or similar bodies, established for the
  management of protected areas as defined in any of the land claim
  agreements;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} renewable resources boards established under any of
  the land claim agreements; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} land use planning bodies established for Wekeezhii or
  any part of Wekeezhii.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 26
- 2014, c. 2, s. 126(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24.1-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 24.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial powers of a board

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** In proceedings before a
board established under Part 3, 4 or 5, the board has the powers, rights
and privileges of a superior court with respect to the attendance and
examination of witnesses and the production and inspection of documents.

### [Financial Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-345982 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual budget

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board shall in each year submit for the consideration of the federal
  Minister an operating budget for the following fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board shall maintain books of account and related
  records in accordance with accounting principles recommended by the
  Chartered Professional Accountants of Canada or its successor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consolidated financial statements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A board shall annually prepare consolidated financial
  statements in accordance with the accounting principles referred to in
  subsection (2) and shall include in them such supporting information
  or statements as are required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Audit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The accounts, financial statements and financial
  transactions of a board may be audited by the Auditor General of
  Canada, who shall make a report of the audit to the board, which shall
  transmit the report to the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Funding

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may establish funding
  arrangements with each board specifying the manner in which funding
  will be made available pursuant to a budget approved by the federal
  Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 26
- 2017, c. 26, s. 62
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** Fees paid pursuant to any
provision of this Act or the regulations shall be deposited to the
credit of the Receiver General.

### [Reports]{.HTitleText2} {#h-345998 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual report

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board shall, within three months after the end of each fiscal year,
  submit to the federal Minister, in such form as the Minister may
  specify, a report on the activities of the board in that year
  including its financial statements for the year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall make the annual report of
  a board available to the public.

### [By-laws, Rules and Other Instruments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346004 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** A board may make by-laws
respecting the conduct and management of its internal administrative
affairs, including by-laws providing for the maintenance at its office
of the minutes of its meetings.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any other provisions of
  this Act, a board may make rules

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting its practice and procedure in relation
    to applications to the board and their disposition, including the
    service of documents, the imposition of reasonable time limits and
    the submission of comments by the public; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for preventing trade secrets and information
    described in section 20 of the [Access to Information
    Act](/eng/acts/A-1) from being disclosed or made public as a result
    of their being used as evidence before the board, including rules
    providing for hearings to be held in private.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before making rules under this section, a board shall
  publish notice of its intention in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in a newspaper circulated in
  the Mackenzie Valley, inviting interested persons to submit written
  representations to the board with respect to the proposed rules within
  thirty days after the publication of notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No further notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where notice is published under subsection (2),
  further notice need not be published if the proposed rules are amended
  solely in response to representations submitted to the board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 3, 5 and 11 of the
  [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) do not apply in respect of
  rules under section 30, a land use plan or amendment to a land use
  plan under Part 2, rules under subsection 49(2), guidelines or
  policies under section 65, policy directions under subsection 50.1(1)
  or 83(1), (2) or (2.1), directions under section 106, policy
  directions under section 109 or 109.1 or subsection 142.2(1) or
  guidelines under section 120.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice in [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice shall be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) immediately after policy
  directions are received by a board, a land use plan or amendment
  thereto is approved, or any other instrument referred to in
  subsection (1) is made or issued, stating that copies thereof are
  available to the public at the main office of the board and at such
  other locations as the board considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, licences and permits issued by
  a board under Part 3 or 4, either before or after the coming into
  force of this subsection, as enacted by subsection 128(3) of the
  Northwest Territories Devolution Act, are not statutory instruments as
  defined in the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 31
- 2005, c. 1, s. 27
- 2014, c. 2, s. 128
- 2015, c. 24, s. 28
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Jurisdiction of Courts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346025 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive original jurisdiction

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 18 of the
[Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7), the Supreme Court of the Northwest
Territories has exclusive original jurisdiction to hear and determine an
application for any relief against a board by way of an injunction or
declaration or by way of an order in the nature of
[certiorari]{.otherLang lang="la"}, [mandamus]{.otherLang lang="la"},
[quo warranto]{.otherLang lang="la"} or prohibition by the Attorney
General of Canada, the Attorney General of the Northwest Territories or
anyone directly affected by the matter in respect of which relief is
sought.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 32
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- 2005, c. 1, s. 28
- 2014, c. 2, s. 129
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Land Use Planning]{.HTitleText1} {#h-346030 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346031 .Subheading}

Definition of [[planning board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** In this Part, [[planning
board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in Land Use Planning Board
or the Sahtu Land Use Planning Board established by sections 36 and 38,
respectively.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part 2

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
46(2), this Part does not apply in respect of lands in a settlement area
that comprise a park to which the [Canada National Parks
Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies, that have been acquired pursuant to the
[Historic Sites and Monuments Act](/eng/acts/H-4) or that are situated
within the boundaries of a local government.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 34
- 2000, c. 32, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guiding principles

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Land use planning for a
settlement area shall be guided by the following principles:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of land use planning is to protect and
  promote the social, cultural and economic well-being of residents and
  communities in the settlement area, having regard to the interests of
  all Canadians;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} special attention shall be devoted to the rights of
  the Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations under their land claim
  agreements, to protecting and promoting their social, cultural and
  economic well-being and to the lands used by them for wildlife
  harvesting and other resource uses; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} land use planning must involve the participation of
  the first nation and of residents and communities in the settlement
  area.

### [Gwich'in Land Use Planning Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346043 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is hereby established, in respect of the settlement area referred to
  in the Gwich'in Agreement, a board to be known as the Gwich'in Land
  Use Planning Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consist of five members including,
  apart from the chairperson, two members appointed on the nomination of
  the Gwich'in First Nation and one member appointed on the nomination
  of the territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Board consists of three members,
  including one of the members appointed on the nomination of the
  Gwich'in First Nation and one of the members not so appointed other
  than the chairperson.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Board shall be located in the settlement area referred to in the
Gwich'in Agreement.

### [Sahtu Land Use Planning Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346053 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is hereby established, in respect of the settlement area referred to
  in the Sahtu Agreement, a board to be known as the Sahtu Land Use
  Planning Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consist of five members including,
  apart from the chairperson, two members appointed on the nomination of
  the Sahtu First Nation and one member appointed on the nomination of
  the territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Board consists of three members,
  including one of the members appointed on the nomination of the Sahtu
  First Nation and one of the members not so appointed other than the
  chairperson.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Board shall be located in the settlement area referred to in the Sahtu
Agreement.

### [Land Use Planning]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346063 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives and other factors

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** The planning board for a
settlement area shall, after consultation with the federal Minister, the
territorial Minister and the first nation of the settlement area,
determine the objectives to be considered and the other factors to be
taken into account in the preparation of a land use plan for the
settlement area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preparation of land use plan

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  planning board shall prepare and adopt a land use plan for submission
  and approval under section 43.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of land use plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A land use plan shall provide for the conservation,
  development and use of land, waters and other resources in a
  settlement area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of land use plan

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A land use plan may include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} maps, diagrams and other graphic materials;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} written statements, policies, guidelines and
    forecasts;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} descriptions of permitted and prohibited uses of
    land, waters and resources;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} authority for the planning board to make exceptions
    to the plan and the manner of exercising that authority; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other information that the planning board
    considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Settlement lands

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A planning board shall take into consideration a land
  use plan proposed by the first nation for its settlement lands in the
  settlement area, and may incorporate that plan into the land use plan
  for the settlement area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public notice

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  planning board shall publish in the settlement area and in adjoining
  settlement areas in the Mackenzie Valley a notice inviting interested
  persons to examine at specified times and places a draft of the land
  use plan prepared by it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public hearings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A planning board may hold public hearings in relation
  to a proposed land use plan after publishing in the settlement area
  and in adjoining settlement areas in the Mackenzie Valley a notice
  specifying the times and places of the hearings and the procedure to
  be followed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 42
- 2014, c. 2, s. 130(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission to first nation and Ministers

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Following the adoption of a land use
  plan, the planning board shall submit it to the first nation of the
  settlement area, the territorial Minister and the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by first nation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a first nation approves a land use plan, it
  shall notify the federal Minister and the territorial Minister in
  writing of the approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Territorial approval

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On being notified pursuant to subsection (2), the
  territorial Minister may approve the land use plan, and in that case
  shall notify the first nation and the federal Minister in writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal approval

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On being notified under subsections (2) and (3), the
  federal Minister may approve the land use plan, which takes effect on
  the date of its approval by the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objections to plan

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a party to which a land use plan is submitted
  does not approve the plan, that party shall notify the other parties
  and the planning board, in writing, of the reasons for not approving
  the plan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration of plan

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} After a planning board has considered any reasons
  provided to it under subsection (5) and made any modifications to the
  land use plan that it considers desirable, it shall submit the plan
  for approval as provided in subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Functions after plan approval

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** Subsequent to the
approval of a land use plan, a planning board shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} monitor the implementation of the plan; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where so authorized by the plan, consider
  applications for exceptions to the plan.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperative planning

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  planning board for a settlement area may cooperate with any body
  responsible for land use planning in any other area, either within or
  outside the Northwest Territories, that is adjacent to the settlement
  area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint land use plans

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A planning board may, in conjunction with a body
  referred to in subsection (1), prepare a land use plan for the
  settlement area and an adjacent area of the Mackenzie Valley, which
  shall be subject to the requirements of this Part in respect of the
  portion of the plan relating to the settlement area.

### [Compliance with Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346109 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First nations, governments and licensing
bodies

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations, departments and agencies of the
  federal and territorial governments, and every body having authority
  under any federal or territorial law to issue licences, permits or
  other authorizations relating to the use of land or waters or the
  deposit of waste, shall carry out their powers in accordance with the
  land use plan applicable in a settlement area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National parks and historic sites

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In particular, measures carried out by a department
  or agency of government leading to the establishment of a park subject
  to the [Canada National Parks Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01), and the
  acquisition of lands pursuant to the [Historic Sites and Monuments
  Act](/eng/acts/H-4), in a settlement area shall be carried out in
  accordance with the applicable land use plan.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 46
- 2000, c. 32, s. 52
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of conformity

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  planning board shall determine whether an activity is in accordance
  with a land use plan where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the activity is referred to the planning board by a
    first nation or a department or agency of the federal or territorial
    government or by the body having authority under any federal or
    territorial law to issue a licence, permit or other authorization in
    respect of the activity; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an application for such a determination is made by
    any person directly affected by an activity for which an application
    has been made for a licence, permit or authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of referral

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The referral or application must be made before the
  issuance of any licence, permit or other authorization required for
  the activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission of decision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A planning board shall transmit its decision to the
  first nation, department, agency, body or person that made a referral
  or application under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 32, a decision of a planning board
  under this section is final and binding.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of plan

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  planning board may, on application or on its own motion, adopt any
  amendments to a land use plan that the planning board considers
  necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption and approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 42 and 43 apply, with such modifications as
  are required, in respect of any amendment to a land use plan.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping and access

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  planning board shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep a public record of all applications made to it
    and all decisions made by it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} furnish, on request and on the payment of a fee
    prescribed under subsection (2), copies of a land use plan or of any
    decision made by it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have the custody and care of all documents filed
    with it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A planning board may, subject to the approval of the
  federal Minister, make rules prescribing fees for copies furnished
  pursuant to paragraph (1)(b), not exceeding the cost of furnishing
  them.

### [Comprehensive Review]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346141 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Periodic review

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** A planning board shall
carry out a comprehensive review of a land use plan not later than five
years after the plan takes effect and thereafter every five years or at
any other intervals agreed to by the federal Minister, the territorial
Minister and the first nation of the settlement area.

### [Policy Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346144 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's policy directions to board

- **[[50.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may, after
  consultation with a planning board, give written policy directions
  that are binding on the planning board with respect to the exercise of
  any of its functions under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Policy directions do not apply in respect of an
  application referred to in paragraph 44(b), a referral or application
  under subsection 47(1) or a proposed amendment to a land use plan
  under subsection 48(1) that, at the time the directions are given, is
  before the planning board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between the policy directions
  given under this section and the provisions of any Act of Parliament,
  any regulations made under an Act of Parliament or any territorial
  law, those provisions prevail to the extent of the conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 131
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Land and Water Regulation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-346154 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346155 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[appurtenant undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[appurtenant undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the work
    described in a licence. ([entreprise en cause]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[authorized user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who uses
    waters without a licence but under the authority of regulations made
    under paragraph 90.3(1)(m). ([usager agréé]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[authorized waste depositor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized waste depositor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    who deposits waste without a licence but under the authority of
    regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(n). ([personne autorisée à
    déposer des déchets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in Land and Water
    Board, the Sahtu Land and Water Board or the Wekeezhii Land and
    Water Board established by sections 54, 56 and 57.1,
    respectively. ([office]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[domestic user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[domestic user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who uses waters

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for household requirements, including sanitation
      and fire prevention;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the watering of domestic animals; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the irrigation of a garden adjoining a
      dwelling-house that is not ordinarily used in the growth of
      produce for a market. ([usager domestique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[federal area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any lands under the
    administration and control of a minister of the Government of Canada
    and any land on which is situated a waste site for which the
    Management --- as defined in the Northwest Territories Lands and
    Resources Devolution Agreement that was made on June 25, 2013 --- is
    the responsibility of the Government of Canada. ([zone
    fédérale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[first nation lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[first nation lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a first
    nation, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} settlement lands of the first nation; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} lands situated within the boundaries of a local
      government and referred to in the first nation's land claim
      agreement as municipal lands. ([terres d'une première
      nation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[instream user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[instream user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who uses
    waters, otherwise than as described in paragraph (a), (b) or (c) of
    the definition [[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, to earn income or for
    subsistence purposes. ([usager ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the surface of
    land. ([terres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a federal area, a type A or type
      B licence permitting the use of waters or the deposit of waste, or
      both, issued by a board under this Part; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to lands outside a federal area, a
      type A or type B licence, or any other licence relating to the use
      of waters or the deposit of waste, or both, issued by a board
      under this Part in accordance with any territorial law. ([permis
      d'utilisation des eaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area in respect of
    which a board has been established, namely,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the Gwich'in Land and Water Board,
      the area described in appendix A to the Gwich'in Agreement;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the Sahtu Land and Water Board,
      the area described in appendix A to the Sahtu Agreement; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the Wekeezhii Land and Water
      Board, Wekeezhii. ([zone de gestion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit for the use of land
    issued by a board under this Part, and "permittee" has a
    corresponding meaning. ([permis d'utilisation des
    terres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to waters, means a direct
    or indirect use of any kind other than a use connected with shipping
    activities that are governed by the [Canada Shipping Act,
    2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15), including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any diversion or obstruction of waters;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any alteration of the flow of waters; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any alteration of the bed or banks of a river,
      stream, lake or other body of water, whether or not the body of
      water is seasonal. ([utilisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any substance that would, to an
    extent that is detrimental to its use by people or by any animal,
    fish or plant, degrade or alter or form part of a process of
    degradation or alteration of the quality of any water to which it is
    added. Alternatively, it means any water that contains a substance
    in such a quantity or concentration or that has been so treated,
    processed or changed, by heat or other means, that it would, if
    added to any other water, degrade or alter or form part of a process
    of degradation or alteration of the quality of that other water to
    which it is added. It includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any substance or water that is deemed, under
      subsection 2(2) of the [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11), to be
      waste;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any substance or class of substances prescribed
      by regulations made under subparagraph 90.3(1)(b)(i);

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} water that contains any substance or class of
      substances in a quantity or concentration that is equal to or
      greater than a quantity or concentration prescribed in respect of
      that substance or class of substances by regulations made under
      subparagraph 90.3(1)(b)(ii); and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} water that has been subjected to a treatment,
      process or change prescribed by regulations made under
      subparagraph 90.3(1)(b)(iii). ([déchet]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[waste site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waste site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as Waste
    Site in the Northwest Territories Lands and Resources Devolution
    Agreement that was made on June 25, 2013. ([décharge
    publique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[water authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[water authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a board or other
    authority having jurisdiction in relation to the use of waters or
    the deposit of waste in any portion of the Northwest Territories or
    Nunavut. ([autorité de gestion des eaux]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[water management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[water management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a water
    management area established by the Governor in Council by
    regulations made under subparagraph 90.3(1)(a)(i). ([zone de gestion
    des eaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any inland waters, whether in a
    liquid or frozen state, on or below the surface of
    land. ([eaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 15, s. 48, c. 25, s. 51
- 2005, c. 1, s. 29
- 2014, c. 2, s. 132
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National parks and historic sites

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part, except sections 78, 79, 79.2 and 79.3, does not apply in respect
  of the use of land or waters or the deposit of waste within a park to
  which the [Canada National Parks Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies or
  within lands acquired pursuant to the [Historic Sites and Monuments
  Act](/eng/acts/H-4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), an authority
  responsible for authorizing uses of land or waters or deposits of
  waste in a portion of a management area excluded by that subsection
  from the application of this Part shall consult the board established
  for that management area before authorizing any such use or deposit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with authority

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A board shall consult a responsible authority
  referred to in subsection (2) before issuing a licence, permit or
  authorization for a use of land or waters or deposit of waste that may
  have an effect in the portion of the management area in which the
  authority is responsible.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 52
- 2000, c. 32, s. 53
- 2005, c. 1, s. 30
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Local government

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part does not apply in respect of the use of land within the
  boundaries of a local government to the extent that the local
  government regulates that use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The board established for a settlement area and the
  territorial Minister shall, in consultation with each local
  government, jointly determine the extent to which the local government
  regulates the use of land within its boundaries for the purposes of
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissemination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A determination under subsection (2) shall be made
  available to the public at the main office of the board and that of
  the local government.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal area --- addition

- **[[53.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Government of Canada becomes responsible for the Management --- as
  defined in the Northwest Territories Lands and Resources Devolution
  Agreement that was made on June 25, 2013 --- of a waste site, the
  federal Minister shall immediately notify the board in writing of the
  lands on which the waste site is situated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal area --- deletion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Government of Canada ceases to be responsible
  for the Management --- as defined in the Northwest Territories Lands
  and Resources Devolution Agreement that was made on June 25, 2013 ---
  of a waste site, the federal Minister shall immediately notify the
  board in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 135
:::

### [Gwich'in Land and Water Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346218 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is hereby established, in respect of the settlement area referred to
  in the Gwich'in Agreement, a board to be known as the Gwich'in Land
  and Water Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consist of five members including,
  apart from the chairperson, two members appointed on the nomination of
  the Gwich'in First Nation and one member appointed on the nomination
  of the territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Board consists of three members,
  including one of the members appointed on the nomination of the
  Gwich'in First Nation and one of the members not so appointed other
  than the chairperson.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Board shall be located in the settlement area referred to in the
Gwich'in Agreement.

### [Sahtu Land and Water Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346228 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is hereby established, in respect of the settlement area referred to
  in the Sahtu Agreement, a board to be known as the Sahtu Land and
  Water Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consist of five members including,
  apart from the chairperson, two members appointed on the nomination of
  the Sahtu First Nation and one member appointed on the nomination of
  the territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Board consists of three members,
  including one of the members appointed on the nomination of the Sahtu
  First Nation and one of the members not so appointed other than the
  chairperson.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Board shall be located in the settlement area referred to in the Sahtu
Agreement.

### [Wekeezhii Land and Water Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346238 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[57.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There is hereby established, in
  respect of Wekeezhii, a board to be known as the Wekeezhii Land and
  Water Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consist of five members including,
  apart from the chairperson, two members who, subject to any agreement
  between the Tlicho Government and an aboriginal people of Canada to
  whom section 35 of the [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/) applies,
  other than the Tlicho First Nation, are appointed by the Tlicho
  Government and one member who is appointed on the nomination of the
  territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister and the Tlicho Government shall
  consult each other before making their appointments.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Board consists of three members, or
  any larger number that is determined by the Board, including one of
  the members appointed by the Tlicho Government or in accordance with
  any agreement referred to in subsection (2) and one of the members
  appointed by the federal Minister, other than the chairperson.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 31
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[57.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57.2 .sectionLabel}** The main office of
the Board shall be located in Wekeezhii.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 31
:::

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346254 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting after expiry of term

- **[[57.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the chairperson of a board is of the opinion that it is necessary for
  a member of that board to continue to act after the expiry of the
  member's term in order for the board to make a decision in relation to
  the issuance, amendment, renewal or cancellation of a permit or
  licence, as the case may be, the chairperson may request in writing
  that the federal Minister authorize the member to act in relation to
  that matter until a decision is made. For the purpose of the
  appointment of a replacement, the office of the member is deemed to be
  vacant as soon as their term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The request shall be made at least two months before
  the day on which the member's term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed acceptance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the federal Minister neither accepts nor rejects
  the request within two months after the day on which it is made, the
  request is deemed to be accepted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 3](#1151720-1173345){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151720-1173345.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- Gwich'in and Sahtu Land
and Water Boards

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** The Gwich'in Land and
Water Board and the Sahtu Land and Water Board shall regulate the use of
land and waters and the deposit of waste so as to provide for the
conservation, development and utilization of land and water resources in
a manner that will provide the optimum benefit for residents of their
respective management areas and of the Mackenzie Valley and for all
Canadians.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 58
- 2005, c. 1, s. 32
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- Wekeezhii Land and Water
Board

**[[58.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.1 .sectionLabel}** The Wekeezhii Land
and Water Board shall regulate the use of land and waters and the
deposit of waste so as to provide for the conservation, development and
utilization of land and water resources in a manner that will provide
the optimum benefit generally for all Canadians and in particular for
residents of its management area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 32
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction --- land

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board has jurisdiction in respect of all uses of land in its
  management area for which a permit is required under this Part and
  may, in accordance with the regulations, issue, amend, renew, suspend
  and cancel permits and authorizations for the use of land, and approve
  the assignment of permits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsurface rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the jurisdiction of a board
  under subsection (1) includes a use of land that is required for the
  exercise of subsurface rights.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 59
- 2005, c. 1, s. 33
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction --- water and waste in
federal area

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board has jurisdiction in respect of all uses of waters and deposits
  of waste in a federal area in its management area for which a licence
  is required under this Part and may, in accordance with the
  regulations, issue, amend, renew and cancel licences and approve the
  assignment of licences.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction --- water and waste
  outside federal area

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A board has jurisdiction in respect of all uses of
  waters and deposits of waste on lands outside a federal area in its
  management area for which a licence is required under any territorial
  law and may, in accordance with that law,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} issue, amend, renew, suspend and cancel licences
    and approve the assignment of licences;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include in a licence any conditions it considers
    appropriate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determine the term of a licence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} determine the appropriate compensation to be paid
    by an applicant for a licence, or by a licensee who applies for an
    amendment or renewal of their licence, to persons who would be
    adversely affected by the proposed use of waters or deposit of
    waste;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} require an applicant for a licence, a licensee or a
    prospective assignee of a licence to furnish and maintain security;
    and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} on the request of a person who is subject to an
    order made by an inspector, review that order and confirm, vary or
    revoke it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension power

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board may suspend a licence in respect of a federal
  area for a specified period, or until terms and conditions specified
  by it are complied with, if the licensee contravenes a provision of
  this Part or a term or condition of the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 15, s. 48, c. 25, s. 60
- 2002, c. 10, s. 178
- 2005, c. 1, s. 34
- 2014, c. 2, ss. 110, 138, 139
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 60"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Considerations

**[[60.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.1 .sectionLabel}** In exercising its
powers, a board shall consider

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the importance of conservation to the well-being and
  way of life of the aboriginal peoples of Canada to whom section 35 of
  the [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/) applies and who use an area
  of the Mackenzie Valley; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any traditional knowledge and scientific information
  that is made available to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 35
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conformity with land use plan ---
Gwich'in and Sahtu Boards

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Gwich'in Land and Water Board and the Sahtu Land and Water Board may
  not issue, amend or renew a licence, permit or authorization except in
  accordance with an applicable land use plan under Part 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conformity with land use plan ---
  Wekeezhii Board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Wekeezhii Land and Water Board may not issue,
