  amend or renew a licence, permit or authorization except in accordance
  with any land use plan, established under a federal, territorial or
  Tlicho law, that is applicable to any part of its management area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 61
- 2005, c. 1, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conformity with Tlicho laws --- Wekeezhii
Board

**[[61.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61.1 .sectionLabel}** The Wekeezhii Land
and Water Board may not exercise its discretionary powers relating to
the use of Tlicho lands except in accordance with any Tlicho laws
enacted under 7.4.2 of chapter 7 of the Tlicho Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 35
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements of Part 5

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** A board may not issue a
licence, permit or authorization for the carrying out of a proposed
development within the meaning of Part 5 unless the requirements of that
Part have been complied with, and every licence, permit or authorization
so issued shall include any conditions that are required to be included
in it pursuant to a decision made under that Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of applications

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board shall provide a copy of each application made to the board for a
  licence or permit to the owner of any land to which the application
  relates and to appropriate departments and agencies of the federal and
  territorial governments.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of applications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board shall notify affected communities and first
  nations of an application made to the board for a licence, permit or
  authorization and allow a reasonable period of time for them to make
  representations to the board with respect to the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Tlicho Government

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Wekeezhii Land and Water Board shall notify the
  Tlicho Government of an application made to the Board for a licence,
  permit or authorization and allow a reasonable period of time for it
  to make representations to the Board with respect to the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Tlicho Government

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Wekeezhii Land and Water Board shall consult the
  Tlicho Government before issuing, amending or renewing any licence,
  permit or authorization for a use of Tlicho lands or waters on those
  lands or a deposit of waste on those lands or in those waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Déline Got'ine
  Government

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Sahtu Land and Water Board shall consult the
  Déline Got'ine Government before issuing, amending or renewing any
  licence, permit or authorization for a use of Déline lands or waters
  on those lands or a deposit of waste on those lands or in those
  waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 63
- 2005, c. 1, s. 36
- 2015, c. 24, s. 29
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heritage resources

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board shall seek and consider the advice of any affected first nation
  and, in the case of the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board, the Tlicho
  Government and any appropriate department or agency of the federal or
  territorial government respecting the presence of heritage resources
  that might be affected by a use of land or waters or a deposit of
  waste proposed in an application for a licence or permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wildlife resources

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board shall seek and consider the advice of the
  renewable resources board established by the land claim agreement
  applicable in its management area respecting the presence of wildlife
  and wildlife habitat that might be affected by a use of land or waters
  or a deposit of waste proposed in an application for a licence or
  permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 64
- 2005, c. 1, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines and policies --- permits

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a board
  may establish guidelines and policies respecting permits and other
  authorizations, including their issuance under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines and policies --- licences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations and any territorial law, a
  board may establish guidelines and policies respecting licences,
  including their issuance under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 65
- 2014, c. 2, s. 140
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of licences and permits

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** A board shall provide the
federal Minister with copies of licences, permits and authorizations
issued under this Part and of decisions and orders relating to them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final decision

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 32
and 72.13, subsections 125(1.2) and (4) and any approval requirement
under any territorial law with respect to the issuance, renewal,
amendment or cancellation of a licence, every decision or order of a
board is final and binding.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 67
- 2014, c. 2, s. 141
- [2019, c. 19, s. 4](#1151722-1173349){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151722-1173349.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public register

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board shall maintain at its main office, in any form that is
  prescribed by the regulations, a register convenient for use by the
  public in which shall be entered, for each application received and
  each licence or permit issued, the information prescribed by the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Register to be open to inspection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The register shall be open to inspection by any
  person during the board's normal business hours, subject to the
  payment of any fee prescribed by the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of contents of register

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A board shall, on request and on payment of any fee
  prescribed by the regulations, make available copies of information
  contained in the register.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 68
- 2005, c. 1, s. 38
- 2014, c. 2, s. 141
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Special Rules for Land Use]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346346 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of the environment

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** Before issuing a permit
for a use of land, a board shall, with respect to conditions of the
permit for the protection of the environment, consult

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the territorial Minister, in the case of land of
  which the Commissioner of the Northwest Territories has administration
  and control;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the minister of the Government of Canada having
  administration and control of the land in the case of any land under
  that minister's administration and control; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the owner of the land, in any other case.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 69
- 2014, c. 2, s. 142
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 69"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation to staff

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** A board may, by
instrument of delegation, specify permits from among a class prescribed
by the regulations that an employee of the Board named in the instrument
may issue, amend or renew and whose assignment the employee may approve.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Posting security

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  board may require, as a condition of a permit or as a condition of the
  assignment of a permit, the posting of security with the federal
  Minister in a form prescribed by the regulations or a form
  satisfactory to the federal Minister and in an amount specified in, or
  determined in accordance with, the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall notify a board of the
  posting of security so required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of security

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where damage to lands results from a permittee's
  contravention of any provision of the regulations or a permit, the
  board may request of the federal Minister that all or part of the
  security posted by the permittee be applied toward the costs incurred
  in repairing the damage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability not limited

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not affect the liability of a
  permittee for any damages to land in excess of the amount of the
  posted security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of security

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall, in accordance with the
  regulations, refund any part of the security posted by the permittee
  that is not applied pursuant to this Part.

### [Special Rules for the Use of Waters and the Deposit of Waste]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346367 .Subheading}

#### [Prohibitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346368 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of waters

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as authorized under the [Dominion Water Power Act](/eng/acts/W-4), and
  subject to subsection (2), no person shall use, or permit the use of,
  waters in a federal area within a water management area except

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the conditions of a licence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as authorized by regulations made under paragraph
    90.3(1)(m).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions from application of
  subsection (1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of the use
  of waters

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a domestic user;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by an instream user; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of extinguishing a fire or, in an
    emergency, controlling or preventing a flood.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties in certain cases

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If any person diverts waters for a purpose set out in
  paragraph (2)(c), the person shall, when the need for the diversion
  has ceased, discontinue the diversion and, in so far as possible,
  restore the original channel conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 72
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit of waste

- **[[72.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except in accordance with the
  conditions of a licence or as authorized by regulations made under
  paragraph 90.3(1)(n), no person shall, subject to subsection (2),
  deposit or permit the deposit of waste in a federal area

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any waters in a water management area; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other place under conditions in which the
    waste, or any other waste that results from the deposit of that
    waste, may enter any waters in a water management area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the deposit of waste
  in waters that form part of a water quality management area designated
  under the [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11) if the waste so deposited
  is of a type and quantity, and deposited under conditions, prescribed
  by regulations made by the Governor in Council under paragraph
  18(2)(a) of that Act with respect to that water quality management
  area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to report unlawful deposits of
  waste

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If waste is deposited in contravention of this
  section, every person who owns the waste or has the charge, management
  or control of it --- or who caused or contributed to the deposit ---
  shall, without delay, in accordance with the regulations, if any, made
  under paragraph 90.3(1)(o), report the deposit to the person or
  authority designated under that paragraph or, if no such person or
  authority has been designated, to an inspector designated under
  subsection 84(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- Tlicho communities

**[[72.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.02 .sectionLabel}** Sections 72 and
72.01 do not apply in respect of a use of waters or a deposit of waste
in a Tlicho community if the local government of that community has
enacted a bylaw providing that a licence is not required for that type
of use or deposit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

#### [Licences]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346398 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[72.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, a board may
  issue, in accordance with the criteria set out in the regulations made
  under paragraph 90.3(1)(c), type A licences and type B licences
  permitting the applicant for the licence, on payment of the fees
  prescribed by regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(k), at the
  times and in the manner prescribed by any applicable regulations made
  under paragraph 90.3(1)(l) or, in the absence of such regulations, at
  the times and in the manner set out in the licence, to use waters or
  deposit waste, or both, in a federal area in connection with the
  operation of an appurtenant undertaking and in accordance with the
  conditions specified in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued under subsection (1) may be issued
  for a term

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of not more than 25 years, in the case of a type A
    licence that is in respect of a class of undertakings prescribed by
    the regulations or a type B licence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of not more than the anticipated duration of the
    appurtenant undertaking, in the case of a type A licence other than
    one described in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific uses

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The board shall not issue a licence in respect of a
  use of waters referred to in subsection 72(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The board shall not refuse to issue a licence merely
  because the use of waters or deposit of waste in respect of which the
  application for the licence is made is already authorized by
  regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(m) or (n).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for issue

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The board shall not issue a licence in respect of a
  federal area unless the applicant satisfies the board that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the use of waters or the deposit of waste
      proposed by the applicant would not adversely affect, in a
      significant way, the use of waters, whether in or outside the
      federal area to which the application relates,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} by any existing licensee who holds a licence
        issued under this Act or any other licence relating to the use
        of waters or deposit of waste, or both, issued under any
        territorial law or the [Nunavut Waters and Nunavut Surface
        Rights Tribunal Act](/eng/acts/N-28.8), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} by any other applicant whose proposed use of
        waters would take precedence over the applicant's proposed use
        by virtue of section 72.26 or any territorial law, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} every licensee and applicant to whom
      subparagraph (i) applies has entered into a compensation agreement
      with the applicant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} compensation that the board considers appropriate
    has been or will be paid by the applicant to any other applicant who
    is described in clause (a)(i)(B) but to whom paragraph (a) does not
    apply, and to any of the following who were licensees, users,
    depositors, owners, occupiers or holders, whether in or outside the
    federal area to which the application relates, at the time when the
    applicant filed an application with the board in accordance with the
    regulations made under paragraphs 90.3(1)(d) and (e), who would be
    adversely affected by the use of waters or the deposit of waste
    proposed by the applicant, and who have notified the board within
    the time period stipulated in the notice of the application given
    under subsection 72.16(1):

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} licensees who hold a licence issued under this
      Act or any other licence relating to the use of waters or deposit
      of waste, or both, issued under any territorial law or the
      [Nunavut Waters and Nunavut Surface Rights Tribunal
      Act](/eng/acts/N-28.8) and to whom paragraph (a) does not apply,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} domestic users,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} instream users,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} authorized users,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} authorized waste depositors,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} persons who use waters or deposit waste, or
      both, without a licence under the authority of any territorial
      law,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} persons referred to in paragraph 61(d) of the
      [Nunavut Waters and Nunavut Surface Rights Tribunal
      Act](/eng/acts/N-28.8),

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} owners of property,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} occupiers of property, and

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} holders of outfitting concessions, registered
      trapline holders, and holders of other rights of a similar nature;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any waste that would be produced by the appurtenant
    undertaking will be treated and disposed of in a manner that is
    appropriate for the maintenance of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} water quality standards prescribed by regulations
      made under paragraph 90.3(1)(h) or, in the absence of such
      regulations, any water quality standards that the board considers
      acceptable, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effluent standards prescribed by regulations
      made under paragraph 90.3(1)(i) or, in the absence of such
      regulations, any effluent standards that the board considers
      acceptable; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the financial responsibility of the applicant,
    taking into account the applicant's past performance, is adequate
    for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the completion of the appurtenant undertaking,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any mitigative measures that may be required,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the satisfactory maintenance and restoration of
      the site in the event of any future closing or abandonment of that
      undertaking.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors in determining compensation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In determining the compensation that is appropriate
  for the purpose of paragraph (5)(b), the board shall consider all
  relevant factors, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provable loss or damage;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} potential loss or damage;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the extent and duration of the adverse effect,
    including the incremental adverse effect;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the extent of the use of waters by persons who
    would be adversely affected; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} nuisance, inconvenience and noise.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

- **[[72.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act and its
  regulations, a board may include in a licence in respect of a federal
  area any conditions that it considers appropriate, including
  conditions

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} relating to the manner of use of waters permitted
    to be used under the licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} relating to the quantity, concentration and types
    of waste that may be deposited in any waters by the licensee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} under which any such waste may be so deposited;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} relating to studies to be undertaken, works to be
    constructed, plans to be submitted, and monitoring programs to be
    undertaken; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} relating to any future closing or abandonment of
    the appurtenant undertaking.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board to minimize adverse effects

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In fixing the conditions of a licence in respect of a
  federal area, the board shall make all reasonable efforts to minimize
  any adverse effects of the issuance of the licence on any of the
  following who would be adversely affected by the use of waters or the
  deposit of waste proposed by the applicant, and who have notified the
  board within the time period stipulated in the notice of the
  application given under subsection 72.16(1), whether they are in or
  outside the federal area to which the application relates, at the time
  when the board is considering the fixing of those conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} licensees who hold a licence in respect of a
    federal area or in respect of lands outside a federal area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} domestic users;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} instream users;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} authorized users;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} authorized waste depositors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} persons who use waters or deposit waste, or both,
    without a licence under the authority of any territorial law;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} owners of property;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} occupiers of property; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} holders of outfitting concessions, registered
    trapline holders, and holders of other rights of a similar nature.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions relating to waste

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a board issues a licence in respect of a federal
  area whose waters form part of a water quality management area
  designated under the [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11), it is not
  permitted to include in the licence any conditions relating to the
  deposit of waste in those waters that are less stringent than the
  provisions of the regulations made under paragraph 18(2)(a) of that
  Act with respect to those waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of regulations under
  [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a board issues a licence in respect of a federal
  area whose waters do not form part of a water quality management area
  designated under the [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if any regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(h)
    are in force for those waters, the board is not permitted to include
    in the licence any conditions relating to the deposit of waste in
    those waters that are not based on the water quality standards
    prescribed for those waters by those regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if any regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(i)
    are in force for those waters, the board is not permitted to include
    in the licence any conditions relating to the deposit of waste in
    those waters that are less stringent than the effluent standards
    prescribed in relation to those waters by those regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Fisheries
  Act](/eng/acts/F-14)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a board issues a licence in respect of a federal
  area whose waters do not form part of a water quality management area
  designated under the [Canada Water Act](/eng/acts/C-11), and to which
  any regulations made under subsection 36(5) of the [Fisheries
  Act](/eng/acts/F-14) apply, it is not permitted to include in the
  licence any conditions relating to the deposit of waste in those
  waters that are less stringent than the provisions of those
  regulations that relate to the deposit of deleterious substances as
  defined in subsection 34(1) of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions relating to works

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The board shall include in a licence in respect of a
  federal area conditions that are at least as stringent as any
  applicable standards prescribed by any regulations made under
  paragraph 90.3(1)(j).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence conditions deemed amended

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If regulations referred to in subsection (3),
  (4), (5) or (6) are made or amended after the issuance of a licence in
  respect of a federal area, the conditions of the licence are deemed to
  be amended to the extent, if any, that is necessary to comply, or
  remain in compliance, with that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inuit-owned land

- **[[72.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A board shall not issue a licence in
  respect of a use of waters or a deposit of waste in a federal area
  that may substantially alter the quality, quantity or flow of waters
  flowing through Inuit-owned land, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant has entered into an agreement with
    the designated Inuit organization to pay compensation for any loss
    or damage that may be caused by the alteration; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there is no agreement,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on the request of the applicant or the designated
      Inuit organization, the board has made a joint determination of
      the appropriate compensation with the Nunavut Water Board, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the board and the Nunavut Water Board are
      unable to jointly determine compensation, a judge of the Nunavut
      Court of Justice has determined the compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The payment of compensation referred to in paragraph
  (1)(b) shall be a condition of the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise determined by the Nunavut Water
  Board, costs incurred by the designated Inuit organization as a result
  of a request referred to in subparagraph (1)(b)(i) shall be paid by
  the applicant.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Negotiation to be in good faith

**[[72.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.06 .sectionLabel}** A board shall not
consider a request referred to in subparagraph 72.05(1)(b)(i) unless the
requester has negotiated in good faith and has been unable to reach an
agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors in determining compensation

**[[72.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.07 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining compensation under paragraph 72.05(1)(b), the following
factors shall be taken into account:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the adverse effects of the alteration of the quality,
  quantity or flow of waters on Inuit-owned land;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nuisance, inconvenience or disturbance, including
  noise, caused by the alteration;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cumulative adverse effects of the alteration and
  of any existing uses of waters and deposits of waste;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cultural attachment of Inuit to the affected
  Inuit-owned land, including waters;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the peculiar and special value of the affected
  Inuit-owned land, including waters; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} any interference with Inuit rights derived from the
  Agreement or otherwise.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Periodic review and payment

**[[72.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.08 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
agreed by the designated Inuit organization and the applicant, a
determination of compensation made under paragraph 72.05(1)(b) shall
provide, having due regard to the nature and duration of the use of
waters or deposit of waste, for the periodic review and periodic payment
of that compensation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

- **[[72.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section and sections 72.05
  to 72.08,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
    [[Inuit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[Inuit-owned
    land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[Makivik]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
    [[Tunngavik]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings as in
    subsection 2(1) of the [Nunavut Waters and Nunavut Surface Rights
    Tribunal Act](/eng/acts/N-28.8); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[designated Inuit
    organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of the jointly owned lands
      referred to in section 40.2.8 of the Agreement,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} Tunngavik, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any organization designated in the public
        record maintained by Tunngavik under the Agreement as being
        responsible for the functions described under sections 20.3.1
        and 20.4.1 of the Agreement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the jointly owned lands referred
      to in section 40.2.8 of the Agreement, Makivik, acting jointly
      with the organization determined under subparagraph (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, sections 72.05 to 72.08 apply
  in respect of a body of water that delineates a boundary between
  Inuit-owned land and other land and that body of water is not located
  entirely on Inuit-owned land.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for licence

- **[[72.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a licence shall be in the form and contain the
  information,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the licence is to apply with respect to a
    federal area, prescribed by the regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the licence is to apply with respect to lands
    outside a federal area, required under any territorial law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information and studies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The board shall require an applicant for a licence to
  provide the board with the information and studies concerning the use
  of waters or deposit of waste proposed by the applicant that will
  enable the board to evaluate any qualitative and quantitative effects
  of the use or deposit on waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security --- federal area

- **[[72.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A board may require an applicant for
  a licence that is to apply with respect to a federal area, a holder of
  such a licence or a prospective assignee of such a licence to furnish
  and maintain security with the federal Minister, in an amount
  specified in, or determined in accordance with, the regulations made
  under paragraph 90.3(1)(g) and in a form prescribed by those
  regulations or a form satisfactory to the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}How security may be applied

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The security may be applied by the federal Minister
  in the following manner:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the federal Minister is satisfied that a person
    who is entitled to be compensated by a licensee under section 72.27
    has taken all reasonable measures to recover compensation from the
    licensee and has been unsuccessful in that recovery, the security
    may be applied to compensate that person, either fully or partially;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security may be applied to reimburse Her
    Majesty in right of Canada, either fully or partially, for
    reasonable costs incurred by Her Majesty under subsection 86.2(1)
    or, subject to subsection (3), subsection 89(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(b) applies in respect of costs incurred
  under subsection 89(1) only to the extent that the incurring of those
  costs was based on subparagraph 89(1)(b)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of security that the federal Minister may
  apply under subsection (2) in respect of any particular incident or
  matter may not exceed in the aggregate the amount of the security
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of security

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any portion of the security that, in the federal
  Minister's opinion, will not be required under subsection (2) shall be
  refunded without delay to the licensee or assignor, as the case may
  be, if the federal Minister is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the appurtenant undertaking has been permanently
    closed or permanently abandoned; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence has been assigned.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal, amendment and cancellation

- **[[72.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  a board may, in respect of a federal area,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} renew a licence, if the licensee applies for its
    renewal or if the renewal appears to the board to be in the public
    interest, with or without changes to its conditions, for a term

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of not more than 25 years, in the case of a type
      A licence that is in respect of a class of undertakings prescribed
      by the regulations or a type B licence, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} of not more than the anticipated duration of the
      appurtenant undertaking, in the case of a type A licence other
      than one described in subparagraph (i);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amend, for a specified term or otherwise, any
    condition of a licence

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the licensee applies for its amendment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the amendment is required to deal with a
      water shortage in any water management area, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, if the amendment appears to
      the board to be in the public interest; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cancel a licence

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the licensee applies for its cancellation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the licensee, for three successive years,
      fails to exercise their rights under the licence, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, if the cancellation appears
      to the board to be in the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 72.03 to 72.11 apply, with any modifications
  that the circumstances require, in respect of a renewal of, or an
  amendment to, a licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to cancel licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application to cancel a licence shall be in the
  form and contain the information that is,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the licence applies with respect to a federal
    area, prescribed by the regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the licence applies with respect to lands
    outside a federal area, required under any territorial law.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval to issue, renew, amend or cancel

**[[72.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.13 .sectionLabel}** A board may issue,
renew, amend or cancel --- in respect of a federal area or lands outside
a federal area --- a type A licence, or a type B licence in connection
with which a public hearing is held by the board with respect to its
issuance, renewal, amendment or cancellation, only with the approval of
the federal Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignment

- **[[72.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any sale or other disposition of any
  right, title or interest, of a licensee who holds a licence in respect
  of a federal area, in an appurtenant undertaking constitutes, without
  further action by the licensee, an assignment of the licence to the
  person or persons to whom the sale or other disposition is made if the
  assignment of the licence was authorized by a board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization of assignment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board shall authorize the assignment of a licence
  if it is satisfied that neither the sale or other disposition of any
  right, title or interest of the licensee in the appurtenant
  undertaking at the time, in the manner and on the terms and conditions
  agreed to by the licensee, nor the operation of the appurtenant
  undertaking by the prospective assignee would be likely to result in a
  contravention of, or failure to comply with, any condition of the
  licence or any provision of this Act or the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence not otherwise assignable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in this section, a licence in
  respect of a federal area is not assignable.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

#### [Public Hearings and Procedure]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346582 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional hearing

- **[[72.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a board is satisfied that it
  would be in the public interest, it may hold a public hearing in
  connection with any matter relating to its objects, including, in
  respect of a federal area or lands outside a federal area,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuance or renewal of, or an amendment to, a
    type B licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amendment to a type A licence under which
    neither the use, flow or quality of waters nor the term of the
    licence would be altered; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cancellation of a type B licence under
    subparagraph 72.12(1)(c)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory hearing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the board shall hold a
  public hearing if it is considering, in respect of a federal area,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuance or renewal of a type A licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amendment to a type A licence under which the
    use, flow or quality of waters, or the term of the licence, would be
    altered;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cancellation of a type A licence under
    paragraph 72.12(1)(c); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the cancellation of a type B licence under
    subparagraph 72.12(1)(c)(ii) or (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, after giving notice of a public hearing under
    section 72.16, the board receives no notification on or before the
    10th day before the day of the proposed hearing that any person or
    body intends to appear and make representations and the applicant or
    the licensee, as the case may be, consents in writing to the
    disposition of the matter without a public hearing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if, in the case of a renewal of a type A licence,
    the licensee has filed with the board an application for renewal in
    accordance with the regulations made under paragraphs 90.3(1)(d)
    and (e) and the term of the renewal or renewals granted by the board
    does not exceed 60 days in the aggregate; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if, in the case of an amendment to a type A licence
    under which the use, flow or quality of waters would be altered, the
    board, with the consent of the federal Minister, declares the
    amendment to be required on an emergency basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of applications

- **[[72.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a board
  shall give notice of each application made to it --- in respect of a
  federal area or lands outside a federal area --- by publishing the
  application in a newspaper of general circulation in the area affected
  or, if there is no such newspaper, in any other manner that the board
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of public hearing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a board shall give notice
  of a public hearing to be held by it by publishing a notice in a
  newspaper of general circulation in the area affected or, if there is
  no such newspaper, in any other manner that the board considers
  appropriate. The day fixed for the public hearing shall be at least 35
  days after the day on which the requirements of this subsection have
  been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public hearing not held

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), if a public hearing is not
  held by a board in connection with an application, the board may not
  act on the application until at least 10 days after the requirements
  of subsection (1) have been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply in respect of an
  application for the amendment of a licence if a board, with the
  consent of the federal Minister in respect of a federal area or in
  accordance with any territorial law in respect of lands outside a
  federal area, declares the amendment to be required on an emergency
  basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- federal area

- **[[72.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A board shall give notice of its
  intention to consider, on its own initiative, the renewal of a licence
  under paragraph 72.12(1)(a), or the amendment of a condition of a
  licence under subparagraph 72.12(1)(b)(ii) or (iii), by publishing a
  notice in a newspaper of general circulation in the area affected or,
  if there is no such newspaper, in any other manner that the board
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- lands outside a federal area

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A board shall give notice of its intention to
  consider, on its own initiative, the renewal, or the amendment of a
  condition, of a licence in respect of lands outside a federal area in
  accordance with any territorial law by publishing a notice in a
  newspaper of general circulation in the area affected or, if there is
  no such newspaper, in any other manner that the board considers
  appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of an
  application for the amendment of a licence if a board, with the
  consent of the federal Minister in respect of a federal area or in
  accordance with any territorial law in respect of lands outside a
  federal area, declares the amendment to be required on an emergency
  basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit --- type A licence and type B
licence

- **[[72.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} With respect to a federal area or
  lands outside a federal area, on an application for the issuance,
  renewal or amendment of a type A licence, or a type B licence in
  connection with which a public hearing is held, or if the board
  intends to consider, on its own initiative, the renewal or amendment
  of such a licence, the board shall make a decision within a period of
  nine months after the day on which the application is made or on which
  notice of the board's intention is published under subsection 72.17(1)
  or (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral to Minister for approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the board decides to issue, renew or amend the
  licence, that decision shall be immediately referred to the federal
  Minister for approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision of Minister and reasons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall, within 45 days after the
  board's decision is referred to him or her, notify the board whether
  or not the decision is approved and, if it is not approved, provide
  written reasons in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time limit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may extend the 45-day time limit
  by not more than an additional 45 days if he or she notifies the board
  of the extension within the first 45 days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence of decision

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the federal Minister does not notify the board
  whether or not the decision is approved within the time limit referred
  to in subsection (3) or (4), whichever is applicable, the federal
  Minister is deemed to have given approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit --- other type B licences

**[[72.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.19 .sectionLabel}** With respect to a
federal area or lands outside a federal area, on an application for the
issuance, renewal or amendment of a type B licence in connection with
which no public hearing is held or if the board intends to consider, on
its own initiative, the renewal or amendment of such a licence, the
board shall make a decision within a period of nine months after the day
on which the application is made or on which notice of the board's
intention is published under subsection 72.17(1) or (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit --- other licences

**[[72.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.2 .sectionLabel}** On an application for
the issuance, renewal or amendment of a licence in respect of lands
outside a federal area --- other than a type A or type B licence --- or,
if the board intends to consider, on its own initiative, the renewal or
amendment of such a licence, the board shall make a decision within a
period of nine months after the day on which the application is made or
on which notice of the board's intention is published under subsection
72.17(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Day on which application is made

**[[72.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.21 .sectionLabel}** An application for
the issuance, renewal or amendment of a licence is considered to be made
on the day on which the board is satisfied that the application is in
the form, and contains all of the information,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the licence applies with respect to a federal
  area, prescribed by the regulations; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the licence applies with respect to lands outside
  a federal area, required under any territorial law.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded period --- information or
studies

- **[[72.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the board requires the applicant
  or the licensee to provide information or studies, then the period
  that is taken by that applicant or licensee, in the board's opinion,
  to comply with the requirement is not included in the calculation of
  the time limit under subsection 72.18(1), section 72.19 or 72.2 or of
  its extension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded period --- environmental
  assessment, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the proposed use of waters or deposit of waste to
  which the application or the licence relates is part of a proposed
  development in respect of which an environmental assessment, an
  environmental impact review or an examination of impacts on the
  environment that stands in lieu of an environmental impact review is
  conducted under Part 5, then the period that is taken to complete that
  assessment, review or examination is not included in the calculation
  of the time limit under subsection 72.18(1), section 72.19 or 72.2 or
  of its extension.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of time limit

**[[72.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.23 .sectionLabel}** A board may suspend
a time limit referred to in subsection 72.18(1) or section 72.19 or 72.2
or its extension

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the board determines that the applicant is
  required to pay compensation, or to enter into a compensation
  agreement, under subsection 72.03(5), until the applicant satisfies
  the board that the compensation has been or will be paid or that they
  have entered into a compensation agreement, as the case may be;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the board is not permitted to issue a licence
  except in accordance with subsection 72.05(1), until the applicant has
  entered into a compensation agreement under paragraph 72.05(1)(a) or
  until a determination of compensation has been made under paragraph
  72.05(1)(b), as the case may be;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the board is not permitted to issue a licence with
  respect to lands outside a federal area except in accordance with any
  compensation requirement under any territorial law, until the
  requirement has been fulfilled; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the board determines that the applicant is
  required to enter into a compensation agreement under section 77 or
  79.1, until the applicant satisfies the board that they have done so
  or the board has determined, under section 79 or 79.3, the
  compensation payable by the applicant.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time limit by federal
Minister

- **[[72.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may, at the
  request of the board, extend the time limit referred to in subsection
  72.18(1), section 72.19 or 72.2 by a maximum of two months to take
  into account circumstances that are specific to the issuance, renewal
  or amendment of the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time limit by Governor in
  Council

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the federal Minister, further extend the time limit extended under
  subsection (1) any number of times.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasons --- decisions and orders

**[[72.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.25 .sectionLabel}** A board shall
issue, and make available to the public, written reasons for its
decisions or orders relating to any licence, or any application for a
licence, in respect of a federal area or lands outside a federal area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

#### [Rights and Duties of Licensees and Others With Authorizations to Use Waters]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346675 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precedence

- **[[72.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If more than one person has a
  licence, or other authorization to use waters issued by any authority
  responsible for the management of waters in the Northwest Territories
  or in Nunavut, in respect of a federal area, the person who first
  applied is entitled to the use of the waters in accordance with that
  person's licence or authorization in precedence over the other
  persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to a licence or
  authorization

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies, with any modifications that
  the circumstances require, in respect of any rights a person acquires
  through an amendment to that person's licence or authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal or assignment of a licence or
  authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a licence or authorization
  that has been renewed or assigned shall, for the purposes of this
  section, be deemed to be a continuation of the original licence or
  authorization.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to sue for compensation

- **[[72.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided by a
  compensation agreement referred to in subparagraph 72.03(5)(a)(ii), a
  person who is adversely affected as a result of the issuance of a
  licence in respect of a federal area or a use of waters or deposit of
  waste authorized by regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(m) or (n)
  is entitled to be compensated by the licensee, authorized user or
  authorized waste depositor in respect of that adverse effect, and may
  sue for and recover any such compensation in any court of competent
  jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights protected

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is not barred from exercising any rights
  conferred by subsection (1) merely because of having been paid the
  compensation referred to in subsection 72.03(5), or because of having
  been paid compensation under paragraph 72.11(2)(a) or under a
  compensation agreement referred to in subparagraph 72.03(5)(a)(ii).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of licences

**[[72.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.28 .sectionLabel}** A board shall
provide the territorial Minister with copies of licences issued under
this Part and of any decisions and orders relating to such licences.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 145
:::

### [Aboriginal Water Rights]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346696 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use without licence

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 72 and 72.01 or any
  territorial law, the Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations have the right
  to use waters or to deposit waste without a licence for purposes of
  trapping and non-commercial wildlife harvesting other than trapping,
  for purposes of transportation related to those activities and for
  traditional heritage, cultural and spiritual purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use without licence --- Tlicho citizens

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 72 and 72.01 or any territorial law
  --- and subject to any applicable Tlicho laws and, in relation to
  waters that are on settlement lands, any limitations under the
  applicable land claim agreement that are of the same type as those
  that apply in relation to waters on Tlicho lands --- Tlicho citizens
  have the right to use water in the part of Monfwi Gogha De Niitlee
  that is in the Northwest Territories without a licence, for purposes
  of wildlife harvesting under 10.1.1 of chapter 10 of the Tlicho
  Agreement, for purposes of transportation related to such wildlife
  harvesting and for heritage, cultural or spiritual purposes of the
  Tlicho First Nation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 73
- 2005, c. 1, s. 39
- 2014, c. 2, s. 163
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive right

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 7.1, the
Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations have the exclusive right to the use of
waters when on or flowing through their first nation lands and to the
deposit of waste in relation to those waters in accordance with the
other provisions of this Part or any territorial law, as the case may
be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 74
- 2014, c. 2, s. 163
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to unaltered waters

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 76 to
78, the Gwich'in First Nation and the Sahtu First Nation have, in
relation to waters when on or flowing through their first nation lands
or waters adjacent to their first nation lands, the right to have the
quality, quantity and rate of flow remain substantially unaltered by any
person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance, amendment or renewal of
licences, etc.

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** The Gwich'in Land and
Water Board or the Sahtu Land and Water Board may issue, amend or renew
a licence, permit or authorization where the use of land or waters or
the deposit of waste proposed by the applicant would, in the opinion of
the board, interfere with a first nation's rights under section 75, if
the board is satisfied that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is no alternative that could reasonably satisfy
  the requirements of the applicant;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} there are no reasonable measures by which the
  applicant could avoid the interference; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence, the requirements of section
  77 are satisfied.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 76
- 2005, c. 1, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Compensation --- Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346717 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for licence

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** The Gwich'in Land and
Water Board or the Sahtu Land and Water Board may not issue, amend or
renew a licence pursuant to section 76 unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and the first nation enter into an
  agreement to compensate the first nation for any loss or damage
  resulting from any substantial alteration to the quality, quantity or
  rate of flow of waters when on or flowing through its first nation
  lands, or waters adjacent to its first nation lands; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or the first nation applies to the
  board for a determination pursuant to subsection 79(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 77
- 2005, c. 1, s. 41
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 77"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to water authority

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Gwich'in Land and Water Board or the Sahtu Land and Water Board
  determines that a use of waters or a deposit of waste that is
  proposed, in an application made to a water authority, to be carried
  out in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an area of the Northwest Territories or Nunavut
    outside the board's management area, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a park to which the [Canada National Parks
    Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies, or lands acquired pursuant to the
    [Historic Sites and Monuments Act](/eng/acts/H-4), in the board's
    management area

  would be likely to substantially alter the quality, quantity or rate
  of flow of waters when on or flowing through first nation lands of the
  Gwich'in or Sahtu First Nation or waters adjacent to those first
  nation lands, the board shall notify the water authority in writing of
  its determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A water authority shall provide a board with such
  information in its possession as the board requires in order to make a
  determination under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any other Act, a water authority that
  is notified by a board under subsection (1) may not authorize the
  proposed use of waters or deposit of waste unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and the first nation have entered
    into an agreement to compensate the first nation for any loss or
    damage resulting from the alteration; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or the first nation applies to the
    board for a determination pursuant to subsection 79(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 15, s. 48, c. 25, s. 78
- 2000, c. 32, s. 54
- 2005, c. 1, s. 42
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral of compensation to board

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  compensation agreement referred to in section 77 or 78 is not entered
  into within the period allowed by the rules of the Gwich'in Land and
  Water Board or the Sahtu Land and Water Board, as the case may be, the
  applicant or the first nation may apply to the board for a
  determination of compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application pursuant to subsection (1), the
  board shall determine the compensation payable in respect of the
  proposed use of waters or deposit of waste, taking into consideration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effect of the proposed use or deposit on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the first nation's use of waters when on or
      flowing through its first nation lands or waters adjacent to its
      first nation lands, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its first nation lands, taking into account any
      cultural or special value of those lands to the first nation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nuisance or inconvenience to the first nation,
    including noise, that may result on first nation lands;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the effect on wildlife harvesting carried on by the
    first nation; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other factor that the board considers relevant
    in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 79
- 2005, c. 1, s. 43
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Compensation --- Tlicho First Nation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346751 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for licence

**[[79.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.1 .sectionLabel}** The Wekeezhii Land
and Water Board may not issue, amend or renew a licence for the use of
waters or the deposit of waste if, in its opinion, that use or deposit
is likely to substantially alter the quality, quantity or rate of flow
of waters when on or flowing through Tlicho lands or waters adjacent to
Tlicho lands unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Board is satisfied that there is no alternative
  that could reasonably satisfy the requirements of the applicant and
  that there are no reasonable measures by which the applicant could
  avoid the alteration; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant has entered into an agreement with the
  Tlicho Government to compensate the Tlicho First Nation for any loss
  or damage that may be caused by the alteration, or the applicant or
  the Tlicho Government has applied to the Board under subsection
  79.3(1) for a determination of compensation for that loss or damage.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 44
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to water authority

- **[[79.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board determines that a use of waters or
  a deposit of waste that is proposed, in an application made to a water
  authority, to be carried out in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Nunavut or an area of the Northwest Territories
    outside Wekeezhii, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a park to which the [Canada National Parks
    Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies, or lands acquired pursuant to the
    [Historic Sites and Monuments Act](/eng/acts/H-4), in Wekeezhii

  would be likely to substantially alter the quality, quantity or rate
  of flow of waters when on or flowing through Tlicho lands or waters
  adjacent to Tlicho lands, the Board shall notify the water authority
  in writing of its determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A water authority shall provide the Board with any
  information in its possession that the Board requires in order to make
  a determination under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other Act, a water authority that is
  notified by the Board under subsection (1) may not authorize the
  proposed use of waters or deposit of waste unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and the Tlicho Government have
    entered into an agreement to compensate the Tlicho First Nation for
    any loss or damage that may be caused by the alteration; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or the Tlicho Government applies to
    the Board under subsection 79.3(1) for a determination.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 44
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral of compensation to Wekeezhii
Board

- **[[79.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a compensation agreement referred to in paragraph 79.1(b) or
  79.2(3)(a), as the case may be, is not entered into, the applicant or
  the Tlicho Government may, after having participated in mediation
  under chapter 6 of the Tlicho Agreement, apply to the Wekeezhii Land
  and Water Board for a determination of compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (1), the Board
  shall determine the compensation payable in respect of the proposed
  use of waters or deposit of waste, taking into consideration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effect of the proposed use or deposit on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the use by Tlicho citizens of waters when on or
      flowing through Tlicho lands, or waters adjacent to Tlicho lands,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Tlicho lands, taking into account any cultural
      or special value of those lands to the Tlicho First Nation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} wildlife harvesting carried on by Tlicho
      citizens;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nuisance or inconvenience, including noise,
    caused by the proposed use or deposit to Tlicho citizens on Tlicho
    lands; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other factor that the Board considers relevant
    in the circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of compensation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The compensation may be in the form of a lump sum
  payment or periodic payments or non-monetary compensation, including
  replacement of, or substitution for, damaged or lost property or
  relocation of Tlicho citizens or their property, or any combination of
  those forms of compensation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 44
:::

### [Cost Recovery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173350 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to pay

- **[[79.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The applicant or a licensee shall
  pay to the federal Minister the following amounts and costs relating
  to the consideration of an application for a licence or for the
  amendment, renewal or cancellation of a licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any amounts that are prescribed by the regulations
    and that are related to the exercise of the powers and performance
    of the duties and functions of a board or of its members;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any costs incurred by a board for services that are
    prescribed by the regulations and that are provided to it by a third
    party; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any amounts that are prescribed by the regulations
    and that are related to the exercise of the powers and performance
    of the duties and functions of the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amounts and costs that a person is to pay under
  subsection (1) constitute a debt due to Her Majesty in right of Canada
  and may be recovered as such in any court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 5](#1151726-1173360){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151726-1173360.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Access to Construction Materials]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346787 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to supply

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Gwich'in or Sahtu First Nation shall supply and permit access to sand,
  gravel, clay and like construction materials situated on its first
  nation lands to any person or any department or agency of the federal
  or territorial government that requests the same where no alternate
  source of supply is reasonably available in the surrounding area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Gwich'in or Sahtu First Nation is entitled to
  fair and reasonable compensation for any construction materials
  supplied or obtained from its first nation lands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Board

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On application by the person or department or agency
  requesting the supply or access, the Gwich'in Land and Water Board or
  the Sahtu Land and Water Board, as the case may be, shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine whether an alternate source of supply is
    reasonably available in the surrounding area; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} resolve any dispute concerning terms or conditions
    of supply or access or priorities between a first nation and other
    users of the construction materials.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Settlement lands outside settlement
  area

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where first nation lands from which construction
  materials are requested are situated outside the first nation's
  settlement area but within the Northwest Territories, the board shall
  consult the resource management authority having jurisdiction in
  respect of those lands before making any determination under
  subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 80
- 2005, c. 1, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to supply --- Tlicho Government

- **[[80.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government shall supply,
  and permit access to, sand, gravel, clay and like construction
  materials situated on Tlicho lands to any person that requests it,
  including any department or agency of the federal or territorial
  government or any local government of a Tlicho community.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the materials are to
  be used on lands other than Tlicho lands, unless no alternate source
  of supply is reasonably available in an area closer to the lands where
  the materials are to be used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation --- Tlicho Government

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government is entitled to be paid for the
  value of materials supplied under subsection (1) and for the exercise
  of a right of access to the materials under that subsection, unless
  the materials are to be used for a public purpose on Tlicho lands or
  in a Tlicho community or for a public road contiguous to Tlicho lands
  or to a Tlicho community.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Wekeezhii Land and Water
  Board

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On application by any person, department, agency or
  government requesting the supply of, or access to, materials under
  subsection (1) and after the applicant has participated in mediation
  under chapter 6 of the Tlicho Agreement, the Wekeezhii Land and Water
  Board shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine, for the purpose of subsection (2),
    whether an alternate source of supply is reasonably available in an
    area closer to the lands where the materials are to be used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} determine, for the purpose of subsection (3),
    whether the materials are to be used for a public purpose on Tlicho
    lands or in a Tlicho community or for a public road contiguous to
    Tlicho lands or a Tlicho community;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} resolve any dispute concerning terms or conditions
    of supply or access, excluding the amount to be paid under
    subsection (3); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} resolve any dispute concerning conflicting uses of
    materials referred to in subsection (1) by the applicant and by the
    Tlicho Government or Tlicho citizens.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Board by Tlicho Government

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a dispute referred to in paragraph
  (4)(d), an application for its resolution may also be made to the
  Board by the Tlicho Government after it has participated in mediation
  under chapter 6 of the Tlicho Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 46
:::

### [Powers and Duties of Federal Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346818 .Subheading}

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014, c. 2,
s. 173\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-81-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 81"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with boards

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** The federal Minister
shall consult the boards with respect to the amendment of this Act or
the making or amendment of any instrument under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 82
- 2005, c. 1, s. 47
- 2014, c. 2, s. 174
- [2019, c. 19, s. 6(F)](#1151728-1173361){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151728-1173361.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-82-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Policy Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346824 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's policy directions to board

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister may, after consultation with a board, give written
  policy directions that are binding on the board with respect to the
  exercise of any of its functions under this Act. The federal Minister
  shall also consult the Tlicho Government before giving such written
  policy directions to the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Déline Got'ine Government

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall inform the Déline
  Got'ine Government, to the extent provided for in 2.7.1 of the Déline
  Agreement, of the Minister's intention to give a written policy
  direction to the Sahtu Land and Water Board if the policy direction is
  in relation to the use of land or water or the deposit of waste in the
  area described in schedule A to the Déline Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policy directions by the Tlicho
  Government to the Wekeezhii Board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government may, after consultation with
  the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board and the federal Minister, give
  written policy directions with respect to the exercise of any of its
  functions under this Part in relation to the use of Tlicho lands.
  Policy directions shall be binding on the Board to the extent that
  compliance with them does not require the Board to exceed its approved
  budget.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policy directions by Déline Got'ine
  Government

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The Déline Got'ine Government may, after
  consultation with the Sahtu Land and Water Board and the federal
  Minister, give written policy directions to the Board with respect to
  the performance of any of the Board's functions under this Part in
  relation to the use of Déline lands. Policy directions are binding on
  the Board to the extent that compliance with them does not require the
  Board to exceed its approved budget.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by subsection (4), policy
  directions do not apply in respect of any application that, at the
  time the directions are given, is pending before a board or has been
  approved by a board and is awaiting approval under section 72.13 or
  under any territorial law, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Policy directions apply in respect of an application
  referred to in subsection (3) if their non-application could result in
  the inconsistency of a licence, permit or authorization with another
  Act or with a regulation or order made under another Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between policy directions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given by the federal Minister under subsection (1) and policy
  directions given by the Tlicho Government under subsection (2) or by
  the Déline Got'ine Government under subsection (2.1), the policy
  directions given under subsection (2) or (2.1), as the case may be,
  prevail to the extent of the conflict.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between legislation and policy
  directions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given by the federal Minister, the Tlicho Government or the Déline
  Got'ine Government under this section and the provisions of any Act of
  Parliament, of any regulations made under an Act of Parliament or of
  any territorial law, those provisions prevail to the extent of the
  conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 83
- 2005, c. 1, s. 47
- 2014, c. 2, s. 175
- 2015, c. 24, s. 30
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-83-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Administration and Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346844 .Subheading}

#### [Designation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346845 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

- **[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister may designate any qualified person, or a class of
  qualified persons, as an inspector to exercise powers relating to
  verifying compliance or preventing non-compliance with this Part and
  orders made under section 86 or 86.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation --- analyst

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may designate any qualified
  person as an analyst for the purposes of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 84
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-84-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 84"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Powers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to enter

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector may, for the purpose of verifying compliance or preventing
  non-compliance with this Part or orders made under section 86 or 86.1,
  enter a place in which they have reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person is using land;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person is using water or depositing waste in a
    federal area within a water management area;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a federal area within a water management area a
    person is constructing any work that, on completion, will form part
    of an undertaking whose operation will require the use of waters or
    the deposit of waste, or altering or extending a work that forms
    part of such an undertaking; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a document or any thing relating to a use or
    deposit referred to in paragraph (a), (b) or (c) is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on entry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The inspector may, for the purposes referred to in
  subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} examine anything in the place;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use any means of communication in the place or
    cause it to be used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} use any computer system in the place, or cause it
    to be used, to examine data contained in or available to that
    system;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prepare a document, or cause one to be prepared,
    based on the data;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} use any copying equipment in the place, or cause it
    to be used;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} take measurements or samples of anything in the
    place;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} remove any thing from the place for examination or
    copying;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} take photographs and make recordings or sketches;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} order the owner or person in charge of the place or
