    any person at the place to establish their identity to the
    inspector's satisfaction or to stop or start an activity;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} order the owner or person having possession, care
    or control of any thing in the place to not move it, or to restrict
    its movement, for as long as, in the inspector's opinion, is
    necessary;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} direct any person to put any machinery, vehicle or
    equipment in the place into operation or to cease operating it; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} prohibit or limit access to all or part of the
    place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall provide every inspector
  with a certificate of designation. On entering any place, the
  inspector shall, if so requested, produce the certificate to the
  occupant or person in charge of the place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall, if it is reasonable to do so,
  give prior notice of their entry to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Gwich'in or Sahtu First Nation, if the entry is
    on its first nation lands;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Tlicho Government, if the entry is on Tlicho
    lands; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Déline Got'ine Government, if the entry is on
    Déline lands.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2019, c. 19, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2019, c. 19, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 85
- 2005, c. 1, s. 48
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
- 2015, c. 24, s. 31
- [2019, c. 19, s. 7](#1151732-1173362){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151732-1173362.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-85-20160901.html "Link to previous version of section 85"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant for dwelling-house

- **[[85.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the place referred to in subsection 85(1) is a dwelling-house, the
  inspector may only enter it with the occupant's consent or under the
  authority of a warrant issued under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to issue warrant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice of the peace may issue a warrant authorizing the inspector who
  is named in it to enter a dwelling-house, subject to any conditions
  specified in the warrant, if the justice of the peace is satisfied by
  information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the dwelling-house is a place referred to in
    subsection 85(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry to the dwelling-house is necessary for the
    purpose of verifying compliance or preventing non-compliance with
    this Part or orders made under section 86 or 86.1; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry was refused by the occupant or there are
    reasonable grounds to believe that entry will be refused or that
    consent to entry cannot be obtained from the occupant.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entering private property

- **[[85.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of gaining entry to
  a place referred to in subsection 85(1), an inspector may enter and
  pass through private property. For greater certainty, no person has a
  right to object to that use of the property and no warrant is required
  for the entry, unless the property is a dwelling-house.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person accompanying inspector

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may, at the inspector's request, accompany
  the inspector to assist them in gaining entry to the place referred to
  in subsection 85(1) and is not liable for doing so.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of force

**[[85.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.3 .sectionLabel}** In executing a
warrant to enter a dwelling-house, an inspector is not permitted to use
force unless the use of force has been specifically authorized in the
warrant and the inspector is accompanied by a peace officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

#### [Orders]{.HTitleText3} {#h-346906 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspector's order --- adverse effects of
land use

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  inspector has reasonable grounds to believe that a use of land has
  resulted in or is likely to result in an adverse effect on the
  environment, the inspector may, in accordance with the regulations,
  order the person who is using the land to take any measures that the
  inspector considers reasonable to mitigate, remedy or prevent the
  adverse effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspector's order --- contravention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an inspector has reasonable grounds to believe
  that a person who is using land is contravening the regulations or the
  conditions of a permit, the inspector may, in accordance with the
  regulations, order that person to take any measures that the inspector
  considers reasonable in order to prevent the contravention from
  continuing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order shall be provided in the form of a written
  notice and shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the reasons for the order; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and manner in which the order is to be
    carried out.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 86
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-86-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 86"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remedial measures

- **[[86.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not a report has been
  made under subsection 72.01(3), an inspector may order a person who is
  using water or depositing waste in a federal area to take any
  reasonable measures that the inspector may specify, including the
  cessation of an activity, to prevent a use of waters, deposit of waste
  or failure of a work from occurring or to counteract, mitigate or
  remedy adverse effects of that use, deposit or failure, if an
  inspector has reasonable grounds to believe

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} waters have been or may be used in contravention
      of subsection 72(1) or of a condition of a licence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} waste has been or may be deposited in
      contravention of subsection 72.01(1) or of a condition of a
      licence, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} there has been, or may be, a failure of a work
      related to the use of waters or the deposit of waste, whether or
      not there has been compliance with any standards prescribed by
      regulations made under paragraph 90.3(1)(j) and with any standards
      imposed by a licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that a danger to persons, property or the
    environment results, or may reasonably be expected to result, from
    the adverse effects of that use, deposit or failure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The order shall be provided in the form of a written
  notice and shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the reasons for the order; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and manner in which the order is to be
    carried out.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to comply

- **[[86.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a person does not comply with an order made under subsection 86(1)
  or (2) or section 86.1 within the time specified, the inspector may,
  on their own initiative, take the measures specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of Her Majesty's costs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any portion of the reasonable costs incurred by Her
  Majesty in right of Canada in the taking of measures under
  subsection (1) constitutes a debt due to Her Majesty recoverable from
  the person in a court of competent jurisdiction or by recourse to any
  security furnished under section 71 or 72.11, as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance to inspectors

- **[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner or person in charge of the place entered under section 85, and
  every person in it, shall give an inspector all assistance that is
  reasonably required to enable the inspector to verify compliance or
  prevent non-compliance with this Part and orders made under section 86
  or 86.1, and shall provide any documents, data or information that is
  reasonably required for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obstruction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to knowingly obstruct or hinder an
  inspector who is exercising their powers or performing their duties
  and functions under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False statements or information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited to knowingly make a false or
  misleading statement or knowingly provide false or misleading
  information in connection with any matter under this Act to an
  inspector who is exercising their powers or performing their duties
  and functions under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 87
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-87-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 87"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review by board

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** A board shall, if so
requested by a person who is subject to an order made by an inspector
under subsection 86(1) or (2) or section 86.1, review that order without
delay and confirm, vary or revoke it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 88
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-88-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 88"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Work closed or abandoned

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister may take any reasonable measures to prevent,
  counteract, mitigate or remedy any adverse effect, in a federal area,
  on persons, property or the environment, and for that purpose may
  enter any place in a federal area, except one that is designed to be
  used and is being used as a permanent or temporary dwelling-house, if
  the federal Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person has closed or abandoned, temporarily or
    permanently, a work related to the use of waters or the deposit of
    waste, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has contravened or failed to comply
      with any condition of a licence or any provision of this Act or
      the regulations, whether or not the condition or provision relates
      to closing or abandonment, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a danger to persons, property or the environment
      may result from the past operation of the work or from its closing
      or abandonment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of Her Majesty's costs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any portion of the reasonable costs incurred by Her
  Majesty in right of Canada in the taking of measures under subsection
  (1), to the extent that the incurring of those costs was based on
  subparagraph (1)(b)(i), constitutes a debt due to Her Majesty
  recoverable from the person in a court of competent jurisdiction or by
  recourse to any security furnished under section 72.11.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 89
- 2005, c. 1, s. 49
- 2014, c. 2, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-89-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 89"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations and Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-346963 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting the use of land

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, following consultation by the federal Minister with first nations
and the Tlicho Government, make regulations respecting the protection,
control and use of lands in the Mackenzie Valley and, in particular, may
make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting uses of land or classes of uses except
  under the authority of permits or, where the regulations so provide,
  under the written authority of an inspector;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the issuance, amendment, renewal,
  suspension, cancellation, and approval of the assignment, of permits;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting eligibility for permits, prescribing the
  conditions or kinds of conditions that a board may include in permits
  and respecting the duration of permits;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} providing for the issuance to permittees by a board
  of authorizations for uses of land not authorized in their permits;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the procedure to be followed and forms to
  be used by applicants for permits, the information to be submitted in
  connection with applications and the manner of its submission, and
  respecting the fees to be paid on the filing of applications;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting fees to be paid by permittees in respect
  of permitted uses of lands belonging to Her Majesty in right of Canada
  or lands that Her Majesty has power to dispose of, other than such
  lands the administration and control of which has been transferred by
  the Governor in Council to the Commissioner of the Northwest
  Territories;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing classes to which permits referred to in
  an instrument of delegation under section 70 must belong;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} specifying the amount, or the manner of determining
  the amount, of the security referred to in subsection 71(1) or
  empowering a board to fix the amount of that security, subject to any
  maximum that may be specified for that purpose, prescribing the form
  and conditions of the security, and specifying the circumstances and
  manner in which it shall be refunded;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form of the register to be maintained
  by a board pursuant to section 72 and the information to be entered in
  it, and respecting the fees, if any, to be paid to examine the
  register or to obtain copies from it;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} respecting the power of inspectors to order the
  taking of measures pursuant to subsection 86(1) or (2);

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} authorizing inspectors to enter and inspect lands to
  which an application relates;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting the restoration of lands to which a permit
  applies;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} authorizing a board or an inspector to relieve
  permittees from specified obligations under the regulations; and

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} authorizing a board or an inspector to require
  permittees to submit reports to them on specified matters.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 90
- 2005, c. 1, s. 50
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- Tlicho lands

**[[90.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.1 .sectionLabel}** Even if the
regulations do not require a permit or authorization under Part 3 or 4
for a particular use of land, no person shall use Tlicho lands without
such a permit or authorization if one is required by a Tlicho law for
uses of that type.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition  --- Déline lands

**[[90.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.11 .sectionLabel}** Even if the
regulations do not require a permit or other authorization under Part 3
or 4 for a particular use of land, a person shall not use Déline lands
without such a permit or authorization if one is required by a Déline
law for uses of that type.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 24, s. 32
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[90.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.2 .sectionLabel}** Despite the
regulations, a permit or other authorization under Part 3 or 4 for a
particular use of land in a Tlicho community is not required if the
local government of that community has enacted a bylaw providing that
one is not required for uses of that type.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 51
- 2014, c. 2, s. 182
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90.2-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 90.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[90.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.21 .sectionLabel}** Despite the
regulations, a permit or other authorization under Part 3 or 4 for a
particular use of lands in the Community of Déline, as described in
schedule B to the Déline Agreement, is not required if a Déline law
provides that one is not required for uses of that type.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 24, s. 33
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- federal areas

- **[[90.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may,
  following consultation by the federal Minister with the Gwich'in and
  Sahtu First Nations and the Tlicho Government, make regulations
  respecting the use of waters and the deposit of waste in federal areas
  and, in particular, may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of the federal Minister and a
    board,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} establishing water management areas consisting of
      river basins or other geo­graph­ical areas, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} classifying purposes of waters use in any water
      management area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of paragraphs (b)
    to (d) of the definition [[waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section
    51,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} substances and classes of substances,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} quantities or concentrations of substances and
      classes of substances in water, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} treatments, processes and changes of water;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} setting out the criteria to be applied by a board
    in determining whether a proposed use of waters or deposit of waste
    for which a licence is required under this Act requires a type A
    licence or a type B licence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} setting out the procedure to be followed on an
    application to a board;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the forms to be used for applications
    to a board, the information to be submitted to a board in connection
    with any application and the form in which any of that information
    is to be submitted;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing forms, in addition to any forms
    prescribed under paragraph (e);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the amount of the security referred to
    in subsection 72.11(1), and prescribing the form and conditions of
    the security, which regulations may empower a board to fix the
    amount of the security subject to a maximum specified in, or
    determined in accordance with, those regulations;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing water quality standards;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing effluent standards;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} prescribing standards for the design, construction,
    operation and maintenance of works related to the use of waters or
    the deposit of waste;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} prescribing fees to be paid for the right to use
    waters or deposit waste under a licence;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the times at which and the manner in
    which fees prescribed under paragraph (k) shall be paid;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} subject to any order made under subsection 91.1(2),
    authorizing the use without a licence of waters in a water
    management area for a purpose or use, in a quantity or at a rate, or
    for a period, or any combination of purpose, use, quantity, rate or
    period, specified in the regulations, and prescribing the conditions
    under which those waters may be used without a licence;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} subject to any order made under subsection 91.1(2),
    prescribing quantities, concentrations and types of waste that may
    be deposited without a licence, and the conditions under which any
    such waste may be deposited;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which a report under
    subsection 72.01(3) is to be made and the information to be
    contained in it and designating a person or authority, in lieu of an
    inspector, to whom the report is to be made;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} requiring persons who use waters or deposit waste
    in a water management area

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to maintain books and records for the proper
      enforcement of this Part, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to submit to a board, on a regular monthly,
      quarterly, semi-annual or annual basis, a report on any of their
      operations to which this Part applies, and specifying the
      information to be contained in it;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} requiring persons who deposit waste in a water
    management area

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to submit representative samples of the waste to
      a board for analysis, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to analyse representative samples of the waste
      and submit the results of the analysis to a board;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} respecting the taking of representative samples of
    waters or waste and respecting the method of analysis of those
    samples;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} respecting the duties of persons designated as
    analysts under subsection 84(2);

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is to be prescribed under
    this Act; and

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} generally, for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Mackenzie Valley

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, following consultation
  by the federal Minister with the Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations and
  the Tlicho Government, make regulations respecting the use of waters
  and the deposit of waste in the Mackenzie Valley

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing fees to be paid

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the filing of any application with the board,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for examination of the register maintained under
      section 68;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the times at which and the manner in
    which fees prescribed under paragraph (a) shall be paid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form of the register to be
    maintained by a board under section 68 and the information to be
    entered in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations may vary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under subsection (1) may vary
  according to any criterion or combination of criteria, including the
  use of waters, the purpose, quantity and rate of that use, and the
  quantities, concentrations and types of waste deposited.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting cost recovery

**[[90.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.31 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, following consultation by the federal Minister with first
nations, the Tlicho Government, the territorial Minister and the boards,
make regulations respecting the recovery of amounts and costs for the
purposes of section 79.4, including prescribing the amounts and services
for the purposes of that section and exempting any class of applicants
or licensees from the application of that section.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 8](#1151736-1173373){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151736-1173373.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting consultation

**[[90.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.32 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, following consultation by the federal Minister with first
nations, the Tlicho Government, the territorial Minister and the boards,
make regulations establishing requirements respecting any consultation
that may be undertaken under this Part, whether or not it is expressly
provided for, by any person or entity with a first nation, the Tlicho
First Nation, the Tlicho Government or an Aboriginal people who uses an
area outside the Mackenzie Valley, including the manner in which it is
to be conducted, and providing for the delegation of certain procedural
aspects of that consultation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 8](#1151736-1173377){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151736-1173377.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

- **[[90.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  regulation made under this Part may incorporate by reference any
  documents produced by a person other than the federal Minister or by a
  body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduced or translated material

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A regulation made under this Part may incorporate by
  reference documents that the federal Minister reproduces or translates
  from documents produced by a body or person other than the federal
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with any adaptations of form and reference that
    will facilitate their incorporation into the regulation; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a form that sets out only the parts of them that
    apply for the purposes of the regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jointly produced documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A regulation made under this Part may incorporate by
  reference documents that the federal Minister produces jointly with
  another government for the purpose of harmonizing the regulation with
  other laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Internally produced standards

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A regulation made under this Part may incorporate by
  reference technical or explanatory documents that the federal Minister
  produces, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifications, classifications, illustrations,
    graphs or other information of a technical nature; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} test methods, procedures, operational standards,
    safety standards or performance standards of a technical nature.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation as amended from time to
  time

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Documents may be incorporated by reference as amended
  from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (5) are for greater certainty and
  do not limit any authority to make regulations incorporating material
  by reference that exists apart from those subsections.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accessibility

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall ensure that any document
  that is incorporated by reference in the regulation is accessible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A person is not liable to be found guilty of an
  offence or subjected to an administrative sanction for any
  contravention in respect of which a document that is incorporated by
  reference in the regulation is relevant unless, at the time of the
  alleged contravention, the document was accessible as required by
  subsection (7) or it was otherwise accessible to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No registration or publication

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a document that is
  incorporated by reference in the regulation is not required to be
  transmitted for registration or published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) by reason only that it is
  incorporated by reference.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** A board may make rules

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying the period of time within which
  compensation agreements referred to in sections 77 and 78 must be
  entered into; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of matters in dispute
  under section 80 or 80.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 91
- 2005, c. 1, s. 52
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Orders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347073 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reservation of lands from disposition

- **[[91.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by
  order, reserve from disposition under any enactment relating to the
  disposition of any lands in a federal area, for a specified period or
  otherwise, all or any interests in such lands if the interests are, in
  the opinion of the Governor in Council, required

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the protection of any waters; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in connection with any undertaking the development
    or operation of which is, in the opinion of the Governor in Council,
    in the public interest and that would require the use of those
    interests in lands and of waters adjacent to those lands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reservation of water rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order and for a
  specified period or otherwise, direct a board not to issue any licence
  in respect of a federal area relating to any waters specified in the
  order, or prohibit a use of waters or a deposit of waste that would
  otherwise be permitted under regulations made under paragraph
  90.3(1)(m) or (n), as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to enable comprehensive evaluation and planning to
    be carried out with respect to those waters; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the use and flow of those waters, or the
    maintenance of the quality of those waters, is required in
    connection with a particular undertaking whose development is, in
    the opinion of the Governor in Council, in the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of contravention of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A disposition of all or any interests in any lands in
  a federal area in contravention of an order made under subsection (1),
  and a licence issued in contravention of an order made under
  subsection (2), is of no force or effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 185
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347087 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal offences --- land use

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who contravenes section 90.1, any provision of regulations made
  under section 90, any condition of a permit or an order of an
  inspector under subsection 86(1) or (2) is guilty of an offence and is
  liable on summary conviction

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a first offence, to a fine not exceeding
    \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six months, or
    to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a second or subsequent offence, to a fine not
    exceeding \$200,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six
    months, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reparation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the penalty provided by subsection
  (1), a court that convicts a person of using land without a permit
  may, taking into account the nature of the offence and the
  circumstances of its commission, order the person to take any measures
  that it considers reasonable in order to repair or limit any damage
  resulting from the act or omission that constituted the offence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2014, c. 2, s. 187\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravening orders

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes subsection 87(1), (2) or
  (3), in relation to the use of land, is guilty of an offence and is
  liable on summary conviction

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a first offence, to a fine not exceeding
    \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six months, or
    to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a second or subsequent offence, to a fine not
    exceeding \$200,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six
    months, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 92
- 2005, c. 1, s. 53
- 2014, c. 2, s. 187
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-92-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 92"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal offences --- water use and
waste deposit

- **[[92.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person is guilty of an offence
  who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contravenes subsection 72(1) or section 72.01;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fails to comply with subsection 72(3); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} contravenes or fails to comply with an order given
    by an inspector under section 86.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is guilty of an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a first offence, to a fine not exceeding
    \$250,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding one year, or
    to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a second or subsequent offence, to a fine not
    exceeding \$500,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding one
    year, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 188
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences --- type A licensees

- **[[92.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every type A licensee who holds such
  a licence in respect of a federal area is guilty of an offence who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contravenes or fails to comply with any condition
    of the licence, if the contravention or failure to comply does not
    constitute an offence under section 92.04; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without reasonable excuse, fails to furnish or
    maintain security as required under subsection 72.11(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every licensee who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a first offence, to a fine not exceeding
    \$250,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding one year, or
    to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a second or subsequent offence, to a fine not
    exceeding \$500,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding one
    year, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 188
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences --- type B licensees

- **[[92.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every type B licensee who holds such
  a licence in respect of a federal area is guilty of an offence who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contravenes or fails to comply with any condition
    of the licence, if the contravention or failure to comply does not
    constitute an offence under section 92.04; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without reasonable excuse, fails to furnish or
    maintain security as required under subsection 72.11(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every licensee who is guilty of an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a first offence, to a fine not exceeding
    \$37,500 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six months, or
    to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a second or subsequent offence, to a fine not
    exceeding \$75,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding six
    months, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 188
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other offences --- water use and waste
deposit

**[[92.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.04 .sectionLabel}** Every person is
guilty of an offence punishable on summary conviction who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} contravenes or fails to comply with subsection
  87(1), (2) or (3), in relation to the use of waters or the deposit of
  waste in a federal area, or any regulations made under paragraph
  90.3(1)(p), (q) or (r); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} except as authorized under this Part or any other Act
  of Parliament, wilfully obstructs or otherwise interferes with a
  licensee who holds a licence in respect of a federal area or any
  person acting on behalf of the licensee in the exercise of any rights
  granted to the licensee under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 188
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offences

**[[92.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.05 .sectionLabel}** An offence under
subsection 92(1), 92.01(1), 92.02(1) or 92.03(1) that is committed or
continued on more than one day constitutes a separate offence for each
day on which it is committed or continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 188
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming --- subsequent offence for land
use

- **[[92.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections
  92(1) and (4), a conviction for a particular offence under this Act is
  deemed to be a conviction for a second or subsequent offence if the
  court is satisfied that the offender has been previously convicted of
  a substantially similar offence under an Act of Parliament --- or an
  Act of the legislature of a province --- that relates to environmental
  or wildlife conservation or protection or heritage resources.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming --- subsequent offence for
  water use

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections 92.01(2), 92.02(2)
  and 92.03(2), a conviction for a particular offence under this Act is
  deemed to be a conviction for a second or subsequent offence if the
  court is satisfied that the offender has been previously convicted of
  a substantially similar offence under an Act of Parliament --- or an
  Act of the legislature of a province --- that relates to environmental
  or wildlife conservation or protection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (1.1) apply only to previous
  convictions on indictment, to previous convictions on summary
  conviction and to previous convictions under any similar procedure
  under any Act of the legislature of a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, ss. 189, 190
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-92.1-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 92.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period or prescription

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** No proceedings in respect
of an offence under section 92, 92.01, 92.02, 92.03, 92.04 or 92.05 are
to be instituted more than five years after the day on which the federal
Minister becomes aware of the acts or omissions that constitute the
alleged offence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 93
- 2014, c. 2, s. 190
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-93-20140325.html "Link to previous version of section 93"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Admissibility of evidence

- **[[93.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  proceedings for an offence under this Part, a certificate, report or
  other document of the federal Minister, a board or an inspector that
  is purported to have been signed by that person or board is admissible
  in evidence without proof of the signature or official character of
  the person appearing to have signed it and, in the absence of evidence
  to the contrary, is proof of the matters asserted in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies and extracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In proceedings for an offence under this Part, a copy
  of or an extract from any document that is made by the federal
  Minister, a board or an inspector that appears to have been certified
  under the signature of that person or board as a true copy or extract
  is admissible in evidence without proof of the signature or official
  character of the person appearing to have signed it and, in the
  absence of evidence to the contrary, has the same probative force as
  the original would have if it were proved in the ordinary way.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumed date of issue

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A document referred to in this section is, in the
  absence of evidence to the contrary, presumed to have been issued on
  the date that it bears.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No document referred to in this section is to be
  received in evidence unless the party intending to produce it has
  provided reasonable notice of that intention to the party against whom
  it is intended to be produced together with a copy of the document.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 190
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of analyst

- **[[93.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, a
  certificate purporting to be signed by an analyst and stating that the
  analyst has analysed or examined a sample submitted to the analyst by
  an inspector and stating the result of the analysis or examination is
  admissible in evidence in any prosecution under this Part and, in the
  absence of evidence to the contrary, is proof of the statements
  contained in the certificate, without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance of analyst

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party against whom a certificate of an analyst is
  produced under subsection (1) may, with leave of the court, require
  the attendance of the analyst for the purposes of cross-examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No certificate is to be received in evidence under
  subsection (1) unless the party intending to produce it has provided
  reasonable notice of that intention to the party against whom it is
  intended to be produced together with a copy of the certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 190
:::

### [Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347179 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Posting of security

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding section
7, Her Majesty in right of Canada and, for greater certainty, the
territorial government shall not be required to post security pursuant
to section 71.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsection
72.03(1) or any territorial law, the Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations,
the Tlicho Government and the Déline Got'ine Government are not required
to pay any fee in respect of the use of waters or the deposit of waste
for non-commercial purposes on their first nation lands, Tlicho lands or
Déline lands as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 95
- 2005, c. 1, s. 54
- 2014, c. 2, s. 192
- 2015, c. 24, s. 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-95-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 95"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board]{.HTitleText1} {#h-347186 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347187 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Part.

  [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Mackenzie Valley Land and
      Water Board established by subsection
      99(1). ([Office]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a federal area, a type A or
        type B licence permitting the use of waters or the deposit of
        waste, or both, issued by the Board under this Part; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to lands outside a federal area, a
        type A or type B licence or any other licence relating to the
        use of waters or the deposit of waste, or both, issued by the
        Board under this Part in accordance with any territorial
        law. ([permis d'utilisation des eaux]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit for the use of land
      issued by the Board under this Part, and "permittee" has a
      corresponding meaning. ([permis d'utilisation des
      terres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms defined in Part 3

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this Part, the expressions [[first nation
  lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
  [[management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meaning as in Part 3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of
  [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, references to a permit
  in section 90, in the regulations made pursuant to that section and in
  sections 90.1, 90.2 and 92 include a permit as defined in subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to "licence"

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a reference to
  "licence" in section 90.3, in the regulations made under that section
  and in sections 72.02, 79.4 and 92.02 to 92.04 includes a
  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 96
- 2005, c. 1, s. 55
- 2014, c. 2, s. 194
- [2019, c. 19, s. 9](#1151738-1173380){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151738-1173380.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 96"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National parks and historic sites

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph 102(2)(b), this
  Part does not apply in respect of the use of land or waters or the
  deposit of waste within a park or park reserve to which the [Canada
  National Parks Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) applies or within lands
  acquired pursuant to the [Historic Sites and Monuments
  Act](/eng/acts/H-4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), an authority
  responsible for authorizing uses of land or waters or deposits of
  waste in a portion of the Mackenzie Valley excluded by that subsection
  from the application of this Part shall consult the Board before
  authorizing any such use or deposit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with authority

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consult the responsible authority
  referred to in subsection (2) before issuing a licence, permit or
  authorization for a use of land or waters or deposit of waste that may
  have an effect in the portion of the Mackenzie Valley excluded by
  subsection (1) from the application of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 97
- 2000, c. 32, s. 68
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Local government jurisdiction

- **[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part does not apply in respect of the use of land within the
  boundaries of a local government to the extent that the local
  government regulates that use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board and the territorial Minister shall, in
  consultation with each local government, jointly determine the extent
  to which the local government regulates the use of land within its
  boundaries for the purposes of subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissemination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A determination under subsection (2) shall be made
  available to the public at the main office of the Board and that of
  the local government.

### [Establishment of Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347220 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board established

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is hereby established a board to be known as the Mackenzie Valley Land
  and Water Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regional panels --- Gwich'in and Sahtu
  Boards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the coming into force of this Part, a board
  established by section 54 or 56 continues as a regional panel of the
  Board under the same name and in respect of the same management area
  as that of the board. Its members become members of the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regional panel --- Wekeezhii Board

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Six months after the coming into force of section
  57.1, the board established by that section continues as a regional
  panel of the Board under the same name and in respect of the same
  management area as that of the board. Its members become members of
  the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicable provisions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the provisions of Part 1
  respecting the appointment, term of office and removal from office of
  members of a board and respecting the chairperson of a board, and the
  provisions of Part 3 respecting the appointment of members of a board,
  the quorum of a board, its main office and members acting after the
  expiry of their term, continue to apply to a regional panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Composition

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the members of the regional panels
  referred to in subsections (2) and (2.1) and a chairperson, the Board
  shall, subject to subsection 108(7), consist of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} two members appointed following consultation by the
    federal Minister with the first nations and the Tlicho Government;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one member appointed on the nomination of the
    territorial Minister; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one other member.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 99
- 2005, c. 1, s. 56
- [2019, c. 19, s. 10](#1151742-1173383){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151742-1173383.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-99-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 99"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual meeting

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** The Board shall hold at
least one plenary meeting in each year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Board shall be at Yellowknife or at such other place in the Mackenzie
Valley as is designated by the Governor in Council.

### [Mandate of Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347241 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- Board

- **[[101.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The objectives of the Board are to
  provide for the conservation, development and utilization of land and
  water resources in a manner that will provide the optimum benefit
  generally for all Canadians and in particular for residents of the
  Mackenzie Valley.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- Gwich'in and Sahtu
  regional panels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The objectives of a regional panel referred to in
  subsection 99(2) are to provide for the conservation, development and
  utilization of land and water resources in a manner that will provide
  the optimum benefit for residents of its management area and of the
  Mackenzie Valley and for all Canadians.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- Wekeezhii regional panel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The objectives of the regional panel referred to in
  subsection 99(2.1) are to provide for the conservation, development
  and utilization of land and water resources in a manner that will
  provide the optimum benefit generally for all Canadians and in
  particular for residents of its management area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 58
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction --- Board

- **[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Board has jurisdiction in respect of all uses of land in the Mackenzie
  Valley for which a permit is required under Part 3 and in respect of
  all uses of waters or deposits of waste in the Mackenzie Valley for
  which a licence is required under Part 3 or any territorial law, as
  the case may be, and for that purpose the Board has the powers and
  duties of a board established under Part 3, other than powers under
  sections 78, 79 and 79.2 to 80.1, as if a reference in that Part to a
  management area were a reference to the Mackenzie Valley, except that,
  with regard to subsection 61(2), the reference to management area
  continues to be a reference to Wekeezhii.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction --- regional panels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A regional panel of the Board shall exercise

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the powers and duties referred to in subsection (1)
    in respect of a use of land or waters or a deposit of waste that is
    to take place, and that is likely to have an impact, wholly within
    the management area of the regional panel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the powers conferred by sections 78, 79 and 79.2 to
    80.1 on the board established under Part 3 for that management area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 102
- 2005, c. 1, s. 58
- 2014, c. 2, s. 195
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 102"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications to Board

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application shall be made to the Board where the application relates
  to a use of land or waters or a deposit of waste

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is to take place, or is likely to have an
    impact, in more than one management area, or in a management area
    and an area outside any management area; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is to take place wholly outside any management
    area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications to regional panel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application relating to a use of land or waters or
  a deposit of waste described in subsection 102(2), including an
  application relating to a licence or permit for such a use or deposit
  issued pursuant to Part 3 before the coming into force of this Part,
  shall be made to the regional panel of the Board for the management
  area referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of panel applications

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A regional panel of the Board shall provide the Board
  with a copy of every application made to the regional panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral between Board and panels

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the Board determines that an application made
  to a regional panel of the Board should have been made to the Board,
  the Board shall dispose of the application, and where it determines
  that an application made to it should have been made to a regional
  panel, it shall refer the application to the regional panel for
  disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions of regional panels

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a decision made by a regional
  panel of the Board on an application is a decision of the Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 103
- 2005, c. 1, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-103-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 103"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of chairperson

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** Applications referred
to in subsection 103(1) shall be disposed of by three or more members of
the Board designated by the chairperson for that purpose, including at
least one of the members appointed to a regional panel --- on the
nomination of a first nation or by the Tlicho Government --- or
appointed to the Board following consultation with first nations and the
Tlicho Government and at least one of the members of the Board not so
appointed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 104
- 2005, c. 1, s. 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-104-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 104"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting after expiry of term

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the chairperson of the Board is of the opinion that it is necessary
  for a member of the Board to continue to act after the expiry of that
  member's term in order for the Board to make a decision in relation to
  the issuance, amendment, renewal or cancellation of a permit or
  licence, as the case may be, the chairperson may request in writing
  that the federal Minister authorize the member to act in relation to
  that matter until a decision is made. For the purpose of the
  appointment of a replacement, the office of the member is deemed to be
  vacant as soon as their term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The request shall be made at least two months before
  the day on which the member's term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed acceptance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the federal Minister neither accepts nor rejects
  the request within two months after the day on which it is made, the
  request is deemed to be accepted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 105
- [2014, c. 2, s. 196](#1151746-1173395){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151746-1173395.html"
  role="button"}
- [2019, c. 19, s. 11](#1151746-1173396){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151746-1173396.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-105-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 105"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board directions

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** The Board may issue
directions on general policy matters or on matters concerning the use of
land or waters or the deposit of waste that, in the Board's opinion,
require consistent application throughout the Mackenzie Valley.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to make recommendations

- **[[106.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall, at the request of
  the federal Minister, make recommendations to the federal Minister
  with respect to the amendment of this Act or the making or amendment
  of any instrument under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretion to make recommendations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board may make recommendations to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister responsible for any Act of Parliament
    regarding the use of land or waters or the deposit of waste, with
    respect to the amendment of that Act or the making or amendment of
    any instrument under that Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the territorial Minister with respect to the
    amendment of territorial laws regarding the use of land or waters or
    the deposit of waste;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a local government with respect to the amendment of
    bylaws enacted by that government regarding the use of land or
    waters or the deposit of waste;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Tlicho Government with respect to the amendment
    of Tlicho laws regarding the use of Tlicho lands or waters on those
    lands or a deposit of waste on those lands or in those waters; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Déline Got'ine Government with respect to the
    amendment of any Déline law regarding the use of Déline lands or
    waters on those lands or regarding a deposit of waste on those lands
    or in those waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 61
- 2014, c. 2, s. 197
- 2015, c. 24, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-106.1-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 106.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cooperation with Other Authorities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347294 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coordination

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** Where a use of land or
waters or a deposit of waste proposed by an applicant for a licence or
permit is likely to have an impact in an area outside the Mackenzie
Valley, whether within or outside the Northwest Territories, the Board
may consult any government, aboriginal group or other body responsible
for the regulation of such uses or deposits in that area and may, with
the approval of the federal Minister, hold joint hearings with or enter
into agreements with any of them for the coordination of activities and
the avoidance of duplication.

### [Powers of Governor in Council and Federal Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347297 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of additional panels

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the federal
  Minister, establish up to two regional panels of the Board in addition
  to those referred to in subsections 99(2) and (2.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The area of the Mackenzie Valley in which such a
  regional panel has jurisdiction shall be specified by the Governor in
  Council, following consultation with affected first nations, which
  area must be wholly outside any management area. Subsections 102(2)
  and 103(2) to (5) apply in relation to that area with such
  modifications as are required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The members of such a regional panel shall be
  appointed by the federal Minister and must include one of the members
  of the Board referred to in paragraph 99(4)(a) and one of the members
  referred to in paragraph 99(4)(b) or (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Chairperson

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The chairperson of such a regional panel shall be
  appointed by the federal Minister from persons nominated by a majority
  of the members of the regional panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment by federal Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a majority of the members do not nominate a person
  acceptable to the federal Minister within a reasonable time, the
  Minister may appoint any person as chairperson.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence or incapacity of chairperson

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A regional panel established under this section may
  designate a member to act as chairperson during the absence or
  incapacity of the chairperson or a vacancy in the office of
  chairperson, and that person may exercise the powers and shall perform
  the duties of the chairperson while so acting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members of Board

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The chairperson and the members of such a regional
  panel who are not already members of the Board become members of the
  Board on their appointment to the regional panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A notice of the establishment of a regional panel
  under this section shall be published in a newspaper circulated in the
  Mackenzie Valley.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 108
- 2005, c. 1, s. 62
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-108-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 108"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's functions

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** The federal Minister
may exercise the same powers and shall perform the same duties in
relation to the Board and its regional panels as are conferred or
imposed on the federal Minister in relation to a board established by
Part 3.

### [Powers of Tlicho Government]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347319 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policy directions by the Tlicho
Government

**[[109.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109.1 .sectionLabel}** The Tlicho
Government may exercise the same powers and shall perform the same
duties in relation to the Board and the regional panel of the Board
referred to in subsection 99(2.1) as those that the Tlicho Government
has under section 83 in relation to the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 63
:::

### [Powers of Déline Got'ine Government]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347324 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policy directions by Déline Got'ine
Government

**[[109.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109.11 .sectionLabel}** The Déline
Got'ine Government may exercise the same powers and shall perform the
same duties in relation to the Board and the regional panel of the Board
referred to in subsection 99(2) as those that the Déline Got'ine
Government has under section 83 in relation to the Sahtu Land and Water
Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 24, s. 36
:::

### [Precedence Relating to Policy Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347329 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between policy directions

- **[[109.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between
  policy directions given by the federal Minister under section 109 and
  policy directions given by the Tlicho Government under section 109.1,
  the policy directions under section 109.1 prevail to the extent of the
  conflict.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between legislation and policy
  directions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given by the federal Minister under section 109 or by the Tlicho
  Government under section 109.1 and the provisions of any Act of
  Parliament, any regulations made under an Act of Parliament or any
  territorial law, those provisions prevail to the extent of the
  conflict.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between policy directions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given by the federal Minister under section 109 and policy directions
  given by the Déline Got'ine Government under section 109.11, the
  policy directions given under section 109.11 prevail to the extent of
  the conflict.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict between legislation and policy
  directions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given by the federal Minister under section 109 or by the Déline
  Got'ine Government under section 109.11 and the provisions of any Act
  of Parliament, of any regulations made under an Act of Parliament or
  of any territorial law, those provisions prevail to the extent of the
  conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 63
- 2015, c. 24, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-109.2-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 109.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost Recovery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173397 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations apply

**[[109.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109.3 .sectionLabel}** Regulations made
under section 90.31 apply to the recovery of amounts and costs relating
to the consideration of an application for a licence issued by the Board
or for the amendment, renewal or cancellation of a licence as if a
reference to "licence" in those regulations were a reference to a
[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 96(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 12](#1151748-1173405){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151748-1173405.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Consultations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173406 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations apply

**[[109.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109.4 .sectionLabel}** Regulations made
under section 90.32 apply to any consultation that is undertaken by any
person or entity in relation to the issuance, amendment, renewal,
suspension or cancellation of a licence, permit or other authorization
by the Board as if a reference to "licence" or "permit" in those
regulations were a reference to a [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or
[[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 96(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 12](#1151748-1173410){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151748-1173410.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347341 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspector

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** An inspector designated
under subsection 84(1) may exercise and shall perform, in relation to
the use of land or waters or the deposit of waste, the powers, duties
and functions of an inspector under Part 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 110
- 2014, c. 2, s. 198
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-110-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 110"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Mackenzie Valley Environmental Impact Review Board]{.HTitleText1} {#h-347346 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347347 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[designated regulatory agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated regulatory agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      agency named in the schedule, referred to in a land claim
      agreement as an independent regulatory agency. ([organisme
      administratif désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[development]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[development]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any undertaking, or any
      part or extension of an undertaking, that is carried out on land
      or water and includes an acquisition of lands pursuant to the
      [Historic Sites and Monuments Act](/eng/acts/H-4) and measures
      carried out by a department or agency of government leading to the
      establishment of a park subject to the [Canada National Parks
      Act](/eng/acts/N-14.01) or the establishment of a park under a
      territorial law. ([projet de développement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[environmental assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[environmental assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      examination of a proposal for a development undertaken by the
      Review Board pursuant to section 126. ([évaluation
      environnementale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[environmental impact review]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[environmental impact review]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      examination of a proposal for a development undertaken by a review
      panel established under section 132. ([étude
      d'impact]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[follow-up program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[follow-up program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a program for
      evaluating

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the soundness of an environmental assessment or
        environmental impact review of a proposal for a development; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the effectiveness of the mitigative or remedial
        measures imposed as conditions of approval of the
        proposal. ([programme de suivi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[impact on the environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[impact on the environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any effect
      on land, water, air or any other component of the environment, as
      well as on wildlife harvesting, and includes any effect on the
      social and cultural environment or on heritage
      resources. ([répercussions environnementales]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} ou [répercussions sur l'environnement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[mitigative or remedial measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mitigative or remedial measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      measure for the control, reduction or elimination of an adverse
      impact of a development on the environment, including a
      restorative measure. ([mesures correctives ou
      d'atténuation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[preliminary screening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preliminary screening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an examination
      of a proposal for a development undertaken pursuant to section
      124. ([examen préalable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regulatory authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regulatory authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a
      development, means a body or person responsible for issuing a
      licence, permit or other authorization required for the
      development under any federal or territorial law, but does not
      include a designated regulatory agency or a local
      government. ([autorité administrative]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[responsible minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[responsible minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a
      proposal for a development, means any minister of the Crown in
      right of Canada or of the territorial government having
      jurisdiction in relation to the development under federal or
      territorial law. ([ministre compétent]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Review Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Review Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Mackenzie Valley
      Environmental Impact Review Board established by subsection
      112(1). ([Office]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part applies in respect of developments to be
  carried out wholly or partly within the Mackenzie Valley and, except
  for section 142, does not apply in respect of developments wholly
  outside the Mackenzie Valley.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 111
- 2000, c. 32, s. 55
- 2005, c. 1, s. 65
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-111-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 111"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal Minister --- powers, duties and
functions

**[[111.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111.1 .sectionLabel}** The federal
Minister shall exercise the powers and perform the duties and functions
of any responsible minister who is a minister of the Crown in right of
Canada for the purposes of subsections 130(1) to (3) and sections 131.2,
135 and 137.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 199
:::

### [Establishment of Review Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347372 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review Board established

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There is hereby established a board
  to be known as the Mackenzie Valley Environmental Impact Review Board
  consisting of not less than seven members including a chairperson.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nominations by first nations and the
  Tlicho Government

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} One half of the members of the Review Board other
  than the chairperson shall be persons appointed on the nomination of
  first nations and the Tlicho Government, including at least one
  nominated by the Gwich'in First Nation, one nominated by the Sahtu
  First Nation and one nominated by the Tlicho Government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government members

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Of the members of the Review Board other than the
  chairperson and those appointed in accordance with subsection (2), at
  most one half shall be nominated by the territorial Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A quorum of the Review Board consists of five
  members, including two of the members appointed in accordance with
  subsection (2) and two of the members not so appointed other than the
  chairperson.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 112
- 2005, c. 1, s. 66
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-112-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 112"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main office

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** The main office of the
Review Board shall be at Yellowknife or at such other place in the
Mackenzie Valley as is designated by the Governor in Council.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting after expiry of term

- **[[113.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the chairperson of the Review
  Board is of the opinion that it is necessary for a member of the
  Review Board to continue to act after the expiry of that member's term
  in relation to an environmental assessment, an environment impact
  review or an examination of impact on the environment that stands in
  lieu of an environmental impact review, as the case may be, of a
  proposal for a development, the chairperson may request in writing
  that the federal Minister authorize the member to act in relation to
  that proposal until the requirements of this Part in relation to that
  environmental assessment, environmental impact review or examination
  have been fulfilled. For the purpose of the appointment of a
  replacement, the office of the member is deemed to be vacant as soon
  as their term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The request shall be made at least two months before
  the day on which the member's term expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed acceptance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the federal Minister neither accepts nor rejects
  the request within two months after the day on which it is made, the
  request is deemed to be accepted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 14](#1151754-1173419){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151754-1173419.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347386 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purposes

**[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of this
Part is to establish a process comprising a preliminary screening, an
environmental assessment and an environmental impact review in relation
to proposals for developments, and

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to establish the Review Board as the main instrument
  in the Mackenzie Valley for the environmental assessment and
  environmental impact review of developments;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to ensure that the impact on the environment of
  proposed developments receives careful consideration before actions
  are taken in connection with them; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} to ensure that the concerns of aboriginal people and
  the general public are taken into account in that process.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guiding principles

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  process established by this Part shall be carried out in a timely and
  expeditious manner and shall have regard to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the protection of the environment from the
    significant adverse impacts of proposed developments;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the protection of the social, cultural and economic
    well-being of residents and communities in the Mackenzie Valley; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the importance of conservation to the well-being
    and way of life of the aboriginal peoples of Canada to whom section
    35 of the [Constitution Act, 1982](/eng/Const/) applies and who use
    an area of the Mackenzie Valley.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration of previous assessment
  activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person or body conducting a preliminary screening,
  an environmental assessment, an environmental impact review or an
  examination of impacts on the environment that stands in lieu of an
  environmental impact review in respect of a proposed development shall
  consider, and may rely on, any assessment activities previously
  carried out under this Part in respect of that development.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 115
- 2005, c. 1, s. 67
- 2014, c. 2, s. 201
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-115-20050804.html "Link to previous version of section 115"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Considerations

**[[115.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115.1 .sectionLabel}** In exercising its
powers, the Review Board shall consider any traditional knowledge and
scientific information that is made available to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 1, s. 68
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75)

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** The [Impact Assessment
Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75) does not apply in the Mackenzie Valley in respect
of proposals for developments other than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} proposals referred to the Minister of the Environment
  pursuant to paragraph 130(1)(c), to the extent provided by that Act;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} proposals that are the subject of agreements referred
  to in paragraph 141(2)(a) or (3)(b), to the extent provided by such
  agreements.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 116
- 2005, c. 1, s. 69
- 2014, c. 2, s. 202
- [2019, c. 28, s. 188](#1158445-1199230){.wb-lbx
