[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transregional impact

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** Where a development
proposed to be carried out wholly in a region of the Northwest
Territories, Yukon or Nunavut adjacent to the Mackenzie Valley, or
wholly in a province, might have a significant adverse impact on the
environment in the Mackenzie Valley, the Review Board may, with the
approval of the federal Minister, enter into an agreement with the
authority responsible for the examination of the environmental effects
of such developments in that region or province to provide for the
participation of the Review Board in the examination of the
environmental effects of the development by that authority.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 15, s. 48, c. 25, s. 142
- 2002, c. 7, s. 207(E)
:::

Version](section-142-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Cost Recovery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173451 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to pay

- **[[142.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The person or body that proposes to
  carry out the development shall pay to the federal Minister the
  following amounts and costs relating to an environmental assessment,
  an environmental impact review or an examination --- carried out by a
  review panel, or a joint panel, established jointly by the Review
  Board and any other person or body --- that stands in lieu of an
  environmental impact review:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any amounts that are prescribed by the regulations
    and that are related to the exercise of the powers and performance
    of the duties and functions of the Review Board or its members or of
    members of the Review Board's review panels or of a review panel or
    a joint panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any costs incurred by the Review Board for services
    that are prescribed by the regulations and that are provided to it
    by a third party; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any amounts that are prescribed by the regulations
    and that are related to the exercise of the powers and performance
    of the duties and functions of the federal Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scope

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the services,
  powers, duties or functions described in that subsection are limited
  to those provided, exercised or performed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} during the period that begins when a proposed
    development is referred to the Review Board under section 125, or
    when a person or body that proposes to carry out a development is
    given notice under subsection 126(5), and ends when a copy of the
    final decision under the process set out in this Part is issued to
    that person or body; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during any period prescribed by the regulations
    that is within the period referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amounts and costs that a person or body that
  proposes to carry out a development is to pay under subsection (1)
  constitute a debt due to Her Majesty in right of Canada and may be
  recovered as such in any court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 30](#1151803-1173465){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151803-1173465.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Public Register]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347891 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public register

- **[[142.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Review Board shall maintain at
  its main office a register convenient for use by the public in which
  shall be entered the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are produced, collected or
    received by the Review Board, a review panel of the Review Board, a
    review panel or a joint panel established jointly by the Review
    Board and any other person or body in relation to any environmental
    assessment or environmental impact review;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any notice it receives under subsection 124(1) or
    (2); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any report it receives under paragraph 125(1)(a) or
    (2)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Register to be open to inspection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The register shall be open to inspection by any
  person during the Review Board's normal business hours, subject to the
  payment of any fee prescribed by the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of contents of register

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Review Board shall, on request and on payment of
  any fee prescribed by the regulations, make available copies of
  information contained in the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Internet access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The register shall also be made accessible to the
  public via the Internet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories of available information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Act, the register
  shall contain information only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it has otherwise been made publicly available; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a record, the Review Board

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} determines that it would have been disclosed to
      the public in accordance with the [Access to Information
      Act](/eng/acts/A-1) if a request had been made in respect of that
      record under that Act at the time the record came under the
      control of the Review Board, including any record that would be
      disclosed in the public interest under subsection 20(6) of that
      Act, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has reasonable grounds to believe that it would
      be in the public interest to disclose the record because it is
      required for the public to participate effectively in a
      preliminary screening, environmental assessment or environmental
      impact review, other than any record whose disclosure would be
      prohibited under section 20 of the [Access to Information
      Act](/eng/acts/A-1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicability of sections 27, 28 and 44
  of [Access to Information Act](/eng/acts/A-1)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Sections 27, 28 and 44 of the [Access to Information
  Act](/eng/acts/A-1) apply to any information described in subsection
  27(1) of that Act that the Review Board intends to be included in the
  register with any necessary modifications, including the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information is deemed to be a record that the
    head of a government institution intends to disclose; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any reference to the person who requested access
    shall be disregarded.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 224
:::

### [Policy Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347916 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's policy directions to Review
Board

- **[[142.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may, after
  consultation with the Review Board and the Tlicho Government, give
  written policy directions that are binding on the Review Board or its
  review panels with respect to the exercise of any of their functions
  under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Policy directions do not apply in respect of a
  proposal for a development that, at the time the directions are given,
  is before the Review Board or one of its review panels.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If there is a conflict between policy directions
  given under this section and the provisions of any Act of Parliament,
  any regulations made under an Act of Parliament or any territorial
  law, those provisions prevail to the extent of the conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 2, s. 224
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347926 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, following consultation by the federal
  Minister with the territorial Minister, first nations and the Tlicho
  Government, make regulations for carrying out the purposes and
  provisions of this Part and, in particular, regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing procedures in relation to preliminary
    screenings, environmental assessments and environmental impact
    reviews generally, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} limits on the time for making any decision or
      recommendation, including a decision of a responsible minister,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the form and content of reports required by this
      Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying the federal and territorial laws in
    respect of which preliminary screenings of proposals must be
    conducted by regulatory authorities and designated regulatory
    agencies before the issuance of licences, permits or other
    authorizations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exempting any development or class of development
    from a preliminary screening for a reason referred to in paragraph
    124(1)(a) or (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form of the register to be
    maintained by the Review Board under section 142.1 and the
    information to be entered in it, and respecting the fees, if any, to
    be paid to examine the register or to obtain copies from it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} fixing a period for the purposes of subsection
    138.1(2) and respecting mediation referred to in that subsection;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} fixing the period within which a matter may be
    referred to arbitration under subsection 138.1(3) and respecting
    arbitration under that subsection;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} fixing a period for the purposes of subsections
    138.1(4) and 141(4);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the recovery of amounts and costs for
    the purposes of section 142.01, including prescribing the amounts,
    services and period for the purposes of that section and exempting
    any class of person or body that proposes to carry out the
    development from the application of that section; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} establishing requirements respecting any
    consultation that may be undertaken under this Part, whether or not
    it is expressly provided for, with a first nation, the Tlicho First
    Nation, the Tlicho Government or an Aboriginal people who uses an
    area outside the Mackenzie Valley, including the manner in which it
    is to be conducted, and providing for the delegation of certain
    procedural aspects of that consultation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Review Board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations may only be made under any of paragraphs
  (1)(a) and (d) to (i), and regulations made under paragraph (1)(b)
  or (c) may only be amended, following consultation by the federal
  Minister with the Review Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with boards established
  under Parts 3 and 4

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In addition, the federal Minister shall consult the
  boards established under Parts 3 and 4 before making or amending any
  regulations under paragraph (1)(i) that relate to any consultation
  undertaken by one of those boards in relation to a preliminary
  screening.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No development is to be exempted for a reason set out
  in paragraph 124(1)(b) if it is a designated project or belongs to a
  class of designated projects, within the meaning of the [Impact
  Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), by virtue of regulations made under
  paragraph 109(1)(b) of that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 143
- 2005, c. 1, s. 90
- 2014, c. 2, s. 226
- [2019, c. 19, s. 32](#1151807-1173477){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151807-1173477.html"
  role="button"}
- [2019, c. 28, s. 169](#1158338-1199249){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1158338-1199249.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-143-20190621.html "Link to previous version of section 143"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference --- limitation
removed

**[[143.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143.1 .sectionLabel}** The limitation set
out in paragraph 18.1(2)(a) of the [Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22) to the effect that a document must be incorporated
as it exists on a particular date does not apply to the powers to make
regulations under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 33](#1151814-1173481){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151814-1173481.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Schedule

- **[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, by regulations made following consultation by
  the federal Minister with the territorial Minister, the Review Board,
  the first nations and the Tlicho Government, amend the schedule by
  adding, or by deleting, the name of any agency, other than a land and
  water board established by Part 3 or 4, that exercises regulatory
  powers pursuant to territorial or federal laws and that is not subject
  to specific control or direction by a minister of the federal or
  territorial government or the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Policy directions of general application governing an
  agency, or the power to approve, vary or rescind an agency's
  decisions, do not constitute specific control or direction for the
  purposes of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 144
- 2005, c. 1, s. 91
:::

[Previous
Version](section-144-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 144"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5.1]{.HLabel1}[Administrative Monetary Penalties]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1173500 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173502 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[144.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 51
    or subsection 96(1), as the case may be. ([office]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person designated as an
    inspector under section 84 or 142.24. ([inspecteur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 51. ([zone de gestion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[penalty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[penalty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an administrative monetary
    penalty imposed under this Part for a
    violation. ([pénalité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[review body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[review body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a violation relating to Part 3
      that is designated as such by a regulation made under paragraph
      144.11(1)(a),

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Gwich'in Land and Water Board, if the
        violation was committed in its management area,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Sahtu Land and Water Board, if the
        violation was committed in its management area,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board, if the
        violation was committed in its management area, or

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board, if
        the violation was committed in an area outside any management
        area;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} despite subparagraphs (a)(i) to (iii), the
      Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board, if the violation relating
      to Part 3 that was committed in a management area is a failure to
      comply with a term or condition of any licence, permit or other
      authorization issued by that Board or a contravention of any
      order, direction or decision made or given in relation to such a
      licence, permit or other authorization; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a violation relating to Part 5
      that is designated as such by a regulation made under paragraph
      144.11(1)(a), the federal Minister. ([réviseur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173518){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173518.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Federal Minister's Powers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173519 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[144.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may, with the
  approval of the Governor in Council and following consultation with
  the Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations and the Tlicho Government, make
  regulations for the purposes of sections 144.12 to 144.31, including
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating as a violation that may be proceeded
    with in accordance with this Act

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any specified provision of
      this Act or of any of its regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any order, direction or
      decision, whether of a specified class or not, made or given under
      this Act, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the failure to comply with a term or condition,
      whether of a specified class or not, of any licence, permit or
      other authorization or development certificate or amended
      development certificate issued under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of or the method of
    determining the amount payable as the penalty, which may be
    different for individuals and other persons, for each violation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} establishing the form and content of notices of
    violation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the service of documents required or
    authorized under this Act, including the manner and proof of service
    and the circumstances under which documents are presumed to be
    served;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the review by a review body in respect
    of a notice of violation; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the publication of the nature of a
    violation, the name of the person who committed it and the amount of
    the penalty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount of penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount that may be determined under any
  regulations made under paragraph (1)(b) as the penalty for each
  violation shall not be more than \$25,000 in the case of an
  individual, and \$100,000 in the case of any other person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173535){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173535.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Violations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173541 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Who may issue notices

**[[144.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.12 .sectionLabel}** Inspectors are
authorized to issue notices of violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173545){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173545.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violation and penalty

- **[[144.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes or
  fails to comply with a provision, order, direction, decision, term or
  condition designated under paragraph 144.11(1)(a) commits a violation
  and is liable to a penalty in the amount that is determined in
  accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of the penalty is to promote compliance
  with this Act and not to punish.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173552){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173552.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of directors, officers, etc.

**[[144.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.14 .sectionLabel}** If a corporation
commits a violation, any director, officer or agent or mandatary of the
corporation who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or
participated in the commission of the violation is a party to the
violation and is liable to a penalty in the amount that is determined in
accordance with the regulations, whether or not the corporation has been
proceeded against in accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173556){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173556.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of violation

**[[144.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.15 .sectionLabel}** In any
proceedings under this Act against a person in relation to a violation,
it is sufficient proof of the violation to establish that it was
committed by an employee or agent or mandatary of the person, whether or
not the employee or the agent or mandatary is identified or proceeded
against in accordance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173561){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173561.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance and service of notice of
violation

- **[[144.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an inspector has reasonable
  grounds to believe that a person has committed a violation, the
  inspector may issue a notice of violation and cause it to be served on
  the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice of violation shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} name the person who is believed to have committed
    the violation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out the relevant facts of the violation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set out the amount of the penalty;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} inform the person of their right to request a
    review with respect to the facts of the violation or the amount of
    the penalty, and of the period within which that right shall be
    exercised;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} inform the person of the time and manner of paying
    the penalty; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} inform the person that, if they do not pay the
    penalty or exercise their right referred to in paragraph (d), they
    are considered to have committed the violation and are liable to the
    penalty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of notice of violation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The inspector shall, without delay after issuing the
  notice of violation, provide a copy of it,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a violation relating to Part 3, to
    the board that may act as the review body and the federal Minister;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a violation relating to Part 5, to
    the federal Minister and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the board that has jurisdiction over a management
      area, if the development is carried out wholly within that
      management area; or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the board established under Part 4, if the
      development is carried out in more than one management area, in a
      management area and an area outside any management area, or wholly
      outside any management area.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173581){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173581.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Rules About Violations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173582 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain defences not available

- **[[144.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person named in a notice of
  violation does not have a defence by reason that the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exercised due diligence to prevent the commission
    of the violation; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reasonably and honestly believed in the existence
    of facts that, if true, would exonerate the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common law principles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every rule and principle of the common law that
  renders any circumstance a justification or excuse in relation to a
  charge for an offence under this Act applies in respect of a violation
  to the extent that it is not inconsistent with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173591){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173591.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing violation

**[[144.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.18 .sectionLabel}** A violation that
is committed or continued on more than one day constitutes a separate
violation for each day on which it is committed or continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173597){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173597.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violation or offence

- **[[144.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Proceeding with any act or omission
  as a violation under this Act precludes proceeding with it as an
  offence under this Act, and proceeding with it as an offence under
  this Act precludes proceeding with it as a violation under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violations not offences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a violation is not an offence
  and, accordingly, section 126 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46)
  does not apply in respect of a violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173604){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173604.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

**[[144.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.2 .sectionLabel}** No notice of
violation is to be issued more than two years after the day on which the
federal Minister becomes aware of the acts or omissions that constitute
the alleged violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173610){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173610.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Reviews]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173611 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

**[[144.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.21 .sectionLabel}** A person who is
served with a notice of violation may, within 30 days after the notice
is served or within any longer period prescribed by the regulations,
make a request to the review body for a review of the facts of the
violation or the amount of the penalty, or both.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173616){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173616.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction or cancellation of notice of
violation

**[[144.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.22 .sectionLabel}** At any time
before a request for a review in respect of a notice of violation is
received by the review body, an inspector may cancel the notice of
violation or correct an error in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173620){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173620.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

**[[144.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.23 .sectionLabel}** On receipt of a
request for a review in respect of a notice of violation, the review
body shall conduct the review.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173624){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173624.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Witnesses

- **[[144.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the review body is a board, it
  may, when conducting its review, summon any person to appear as a
  witness and order the witness to give evidence orally or in writing
  and produce any documents and things that it considers necessary for
  the purpose of the review.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of summonses and orders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any summons issued or order made under subsection (1)
  may be made a summons or an order of the Federal Court or of the
  superior court of a province and is enforceable in the same manner as
  a summons or an order of that court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} To make a summons issued or an order made under
  subsection (1) a summons or an order of the Federal Court or of the
  superior court of a province, the usual practice and procedure of the
  court in such matters may be followed or a certified copy of the
  summons or order may be filed with the court's registrar and the
  summons or order then becomes a summons or an order of that court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees for witnesses

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A witness who is served with a summons is entitled to
  receive the fees and allowances to which persons who are summoned to
  appear as witnesses before the Federal Court are entitled.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173635){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173635.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

- **[[144.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The review body shall determine, as
  the case may be, whether the person committed the violation or whether
  the amount of the penalty for the violation was determined in
  accordance with the regulations, or both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the review body determines that the amount of the
  penalty for the violation was not determined in accordance with the
  regulations, the review body shall correct it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of facts --- burden of proof

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the facts of a violation are reviewed, the
  inspector who issued the notice of violation shall establish, on a
  balance of probabilities, that the person named in it committed the
  violation identified in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written reasons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The review body shall make a determination in
  writing, with reasons, and cause it to be served on the person who
  requested the review.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination final --- board

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A determination made under this section by a board
  acting as a review body is final and binding and, except for judicial
  review under section 32, is not subject to appeal or to review by any
  court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination final --- federal
  Minister

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A determination made under this section by the
  federal Minister acting as a review body is final and binding and,
  except for judicial review under the [Federal Courts
  Act](/eng/acts/F-7), is not subject to appeal or to review by any
  court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the review body determines that the person who
  requested the review committed the violation, the person who requested
  the review is liable to the penalty as set out in the determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of determination

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The review body shall, without delay after making the
  determination, provide a copy of it to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the federal Minister, if the review body is a
    board; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the board to which a copy of the notice of
    violation was provided under subsection 144.16(3), if the review
    body is the federal Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173656){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173656.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Responsibility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173657 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

**[[144.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.26 .sectionLabel}** If a person pays
the penalty set out in a notice of violation, the person is considered
to have committed the violation and proceedings in respect of it are
ended.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173661){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173661.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to act

**[[144.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.27 .sectionLabel}** A person who
neither pays the penalty within the period set out in the notice of
violation --- nor requests a review within the period referred to in
section 144.21 --- is considered to have committed the violation and is
liable to the penalty.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173665){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173665.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Recovery of Penalties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173666 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

- **[[144.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A penalty constitutes a debt due to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada and may be recovered as such in any
  court of competent jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period or prescription

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No proceedings to recover the debt are to be
  instituted more than five years after the day on which the debt
  becomes payable.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173673){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173673.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[144.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.29
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may issue a
  certificate of non-payment certifying the unpaid amount of any debt
  referred to in subsection 144.28(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Registration in any court of competent jurisdiction
  of a certificate of non-payment has the same effect as a judgment of
  that court for a debt of the amount specified in the certificate and
  all related registration costs.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173680){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173680.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173681 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authenticity of documents

**[[144.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.3 .sectionLabel}** In the absence of
evidence to the contrary, a document that appears to be a notice issued
under subsection 144.16(1) is presumed to be authentic and is proof of
its contents in any proceeding in respect of a violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173685){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173685.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication related to violation

- **[[144.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and any
  regulations, the board that received a copy of the notice of violation
  under subsection 144.16(3) --- whether acting as a review body or not
  --- may make public the nature of a violation, the name of the person
  who committed it and the amount of the penalty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction of publication

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The board may only make that information public if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person named in the notice of violation has
    paid the penalty set out in the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person has not requested a review within the
    period referred to in section 144.21; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the review body has determined that the person who
    requested the review committed the violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173695){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173695.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 5.2]{.HLabel1}[Regional Studies]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1173696 .Part}

### [Committee Studying Impact of Works and Activities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173697 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment

- **[[144.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may establish a
  committee to conduct a study of the impact of existing or future works
  or activities carried out in a region of the Mackenzie Valley.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall appoint one or more
  persons as members of the committee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall establish the committee's
  terms of reference after seeking and considering the advice of the
  territorial government and, if the study examines works or activities
  affecting any first nation or the Tlicho First Nation, the advice of
  that first nation or the Tlicho Government, as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173713){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173713.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest

- **[[144.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not be appointed, or
  continue, as a member of the committee if doing so would place them in
  a material conflict of interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status or entitlements under agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is not placed in a material conflict of
  interest merely because of any status or entitlement conferred on them
  under the Gwich'in Agreement, the Sahtu Agreement, the Tlicho
  Agreement or any other agreement between a first nation and Her
  Majesty in right of Canada for the settlement of a land claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173723){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173723.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other participants in study

**[[144.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.34 .sectionLabel}** The federal
Minister, if he or she considers it appropriate, may enter into an
agreement or arrangement with any person or body that possesses
knowledge or expertise that is relevant to the study to participate in
the study.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173730){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173730.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elements to consider

**[[144.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.35 .sectionLabel}** In conducting its
study, the committee shall consider any traditional knowledge and
scientific information that is made available to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173734){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173734.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[144.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.36 .sectionLabel}** Subject to any
other federal or territorial law, the committee may obtain, from any
board established by this Act or from any department or agency of the
federal or territorial government, any information in the possession of
the board, department or agency that is required for it to conduct its
study.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173741){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173741.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Joint Committee]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173742 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment

**[[144.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.37 .sectionLabel}** If the federal
Minister is of the opinion that it is appropriate to conduct a study of
the impact of existing or future works or activities carried out in a
region of the Mackenzie Valley and in a region contiguous to it, the
federal Minister may enter into an agreement or arrangement with an
authority responsible for the examination of environmental effects in
that region respecting the establishment of a joint committee to conduct
the study and the manner in which the study is to be conducted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173746){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173746.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Report]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1173747 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to federal Minister

**[[144.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.38 .sectionLabel}** On completion of
its study, the committee or joint committee shall provide a report to
the federal Minister, who shall make it available to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173751){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173751.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration of report

**[[144.39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144.39 .sectionLabel}** The report must
be considered in the exercise or performance under this Act of any
powers, duties or functions of the boards established under subsections
36(1), 38(1), 54(1), 56(1), 57.1(1) and 99(1), the Mackenzie Valley
Environmental Impact Review Board, its review panels, or a review panel
or a joint panel established jointly by the Review Board and any other
person or body, and of any body conducting a preliminary screening of a
proposal for a development under section 124.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 19, s. 35](#1151818-1173755){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151818-1173755.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Environmental Monitoring and Audit]{.HTitleText1} {#h-347952 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[impact on the environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[impact on the environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in Part 5. ([répercussions
    environnementales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[responsible authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[responsible authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person or
    body designated by the regulations as the responsible authority or,
    in the absence of a designation, the federal Minister. ([autorité
    compétente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cumulative environmental impact

**[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** The responsible
authority shall, subject to the regulations, analyze data collected by
it, scientific data, traditional knowledge and other pertinent
information for the purpose of monitoring the cumulative impact on the
environment of concurrent and sequential uses of land and water and
deposits of waste in the Mackenzie Valley.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with first nations and
Tlicho Government

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  responsible authority that is a minister of the Crown in right of
  Canada shall carry out the functions referred to in section 146 in
  consultation with the first nations and the Tlicho Government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Role of first nations and Tlicho
  Government

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a responsible authority is other than a
  minister of the Crown in right of Canada, the Gwich'in and Sahtu First
  Nations and the Tlicho Government are entitled to participate in the
  functions referred to in section 146 in the manner provided by the
  regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 147
- 2005, c. 1, s. 92
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-147-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 147"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Environmental audit

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  federal Minister shall have an environmental audit conducted at least
  once every five years by a person or body that is independent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms of reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall, after consulting the
  Gwich'in First Nation, the Sahtu First Nation, the Tlicho Government
  and the territorial government, fix the terms of reference of an
  environmental audit, including the key components of the environment
  to be examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of audit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An environmental audit shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of information, including information
    collected or analyzed under section 146, in order to determine
    trends in environmental quality, potential contributing factors to
    changes in the environment and the significance of those trends;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a review of the effectiveness of methods used for
    carrying out the functions referred to in section 146;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a review of the effectiveness of the regulation of
    uses of land and water and deposits of waste on the protection of
    the key components of the environment from significant adverse
    impact; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a review of the response to any recommendations of
    previous environmental audits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of audit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A report of the environmental audit, which may
  include recommendations, shall be prepared and submitted to the
  federal Minister, who shall make the report available to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation by first nations and
  Tlicho Government

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations and the Tlicho
  Government are entitled to participate in an environmental audit in
  the manner provided by the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 148
- 2005, c. 1, s. 93
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-148-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 148"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** Subject to any other
federal or territorial law, a responsible authority or a person or body
who performs an environmental audit may obtain, from any board
established by this Act or from any department or agency of the federal
or territorial government, any information in the possession of the
board, department or agency that is required for the performance of the
functions of the responsible authority or person under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, after consultation by the federal Minister with affected first
nations, the Tlicho Government and the territorial Minister, make
regulations for carrying out the purposes and provisions of this Part
and, in particular, regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the collection of data and the analysis of
  data so collected and scientific data, traditional knowledge and other
  information, for the purposes of section 146;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} designating a person or body as the responsible
  authority for the purposes of this Part; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of participation of the
  Gwich'in and Sahtu First Nations and the Tlicho Government in the
  functions of a responsible authority that is not a minister of the
  Crown or in an environmental audit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 150
- 2005, c. 1, s. 94
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-150-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 150"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions, Consequential Amendments and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-347992 .Part}

### [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-347993 .Subheading}

**[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014, c.
2, s. 232\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-151-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 151"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Existing rights and interests

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** Rights to the use of
land under any lease, easement or other interest in land that was
granted under the [Territorial Lands Act](/eng/acts/T-7) or the
regulations made under that Act, or under any territorial law, and that
exist on December 22, 1998, with respect to a settlement area, or on
March 31, 2000, with respect to any other portion of the Mackenzie
Valley, continue in effect, subject to the terms and conditions of
exercising those rights.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 152
- 2014, c. 2, s. 233
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 152"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Existing licences continued

**[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** Licences issued under
the [Northwest Territories Waters Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3) respecting a
use of waters or deposit of waste in a settlement area that exist on
December 22, 1998 --- and, respecting a use of waters or deposit of
waste in another portion of the Mackenzie Valley, that exist on March
31, 2000 --- continue in effect and are deemed to be licences within the
meaning of Part 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 153
- 2014, c. 2, s. 233
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 153"){.PITLink}
:::

2, s. 234\]]{.Repealed}

2, s. 234\]]{.Repealed}

**[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014, c.
2, s. 234\]]{.Repealed}

[Previous
Version](section-156-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 156"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspectors

- **[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person acting, before the coming into force of section 84, as an
  inspector for the purposes of regulations made pursuant to the
  [Territorial Lands Act](/eng/acts/T-7) is deemed to be an inspector
  designated under that section.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2014, c. 2, s. 235\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 25, s. 157
- 2014, c. 2, s. 235
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-157-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 157"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part 5

**[[157.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157.1 .sectionLabel}** Part 5 does not
apply in respect of any licence, permit or other authorization related
to an undertaking that is the subject of a licence or permit issued
before June 22, 1984, except a licence, permit or other authorization
for an abandonment, decommissioning or other significant alteration of
the project.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2005, c. 1, s. 95

  - ###### Wekeezhii Land and Water Board {#wekeezhii-land-and-water-board .MarginalNote}

    - [95]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Wekeezhii Land and Water
      Board established by section 57.1 of the [Mackenzie Valley
      Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2), as enacted by section
      31 of this Act, may not exercise its powers or perform its duties
      under sections 58.1 and 59, subsections 60(1) and (2), sections
      79.1 to 79.3, 80.1 and 88 and subsection 89(2) of the [Mackenzie
      Valley Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2) until six months
      after the coming into force of this Act.

    - ###### Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board {#mackenzie-valley-land-and-water-board .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 102(1) of the [Mackenzie
      Valley Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2), the Mackenzie
      Valley Land and Water Board shall exercise the powers and perform
      the duties of the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board under sections
      58.1, 79.1 to 79.3, 80.1 and 88 and subsection 89(2) of that Act
      during the period of six months after the coming into force of
      this Act.

    - ###### Exclusive original jurisdiction {#exclusive-original-jurisdiction .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 32(1) of the [Mackenzie Valley
      Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2) and section 18 of the
      [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7), the Supreme Court of the
      Northwest Territories has exclusive original jurisdiction to hear
      and determine any action or proceeding, whether or not by way of
      an application of a type referred to in subsection 32(1) of the
      [Mackenzie Valley Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2),
      concerning the jurisdiction of the Wekeezhii Land and Water Board
      during the period of six months after the coming into force of
      this Act.

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 241

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions .MarginalNote}

    [241]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section and
    in sections 242 to 252.

    [[other Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[other Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Mackenzie Valley
        Resource Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2). ([autre
        loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[regional panels]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[regional panels]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Gwich'in Land
        and Water Board, the Sahtu Land and Water Board and the
        Wekeezhii Land and Water Board that are continued as regional
        panels of the Mackenzie Valley Land and Water Board by
        subsections 99(2) and (2.1) of the other Act, as those
        subsections read immediately before the day on which section 193
        comes into force. ([formations régionales]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 247

  - ###### Designation {#designation .MarginalNote}

    [247]{.lawlabel} Any person who, immediately before the day on which
    section 177 comes into force, is designated as an inspector or
    analyst under subsection 35(1) of the [Northwest Territories Waters
    Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3), is deemed, as of that day, to be designated
    as an inspector or analyst under section 84(1) or (2), respectively,
    of the other Act, as that subsection reads on that day.

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 248

  - ###### Remedial measures {#remedial-measures .MarginalNote}

    [248]{.lawlabel} Any direction that was given by an inspector under
    subsection 37(1) of the [Northwest Territories Waters
    Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3) immediately before the day on which section
    177 comes into force, and that is in respect of lands in the
    Mackenzie Valley that are in a federal area as defined in section 51
    of the other Act as that section reads on that day, is deemed on
    that day to be an order made under subsection 86.1(1) of the other
    Act, as that subsection reads on that day.

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 249

  - ###### Reservation of lands from disposition {#reservation-of-lands-from-disposition .MarginalNote}

    - [249]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any order that was made by the
      Governor in Council under subsection 34(1) of the [Northwest
      Territories Waters Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3), before the day on which
      section 185 comes into force, and that is in respect of interests
      in lands in the Mackenzie Valley that are in a federal area as
      defined in section 51 of the other Act as it reads on that day, is
      deemed, on that day, to be an order made under subsection 91.1(1)
      of the other Act, as enacted by that section 185.

    - ###### Reservation of water rights {#reservation-of-water-rights .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any order that was made by the Governor in
      Council under subsection 34(2) of the [Northwest Territories
      Waters Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3), before the day on which section 185
      comes into force, and that was in respect of lands in the
      Mackenzie Valley that are in a federal area, as defined in section
      51 of the other Act as it reads on that day, is deemed, on that
      day, to be an order made under subsection 91.1(2) of the other
      Act, as enacted by that section 185.

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 250

  - ###### Time limits {#time-limits .MarginalNote}

    [250]{.lawlabel} Any time limit that is applicable --- on the day on
    which section 206 comes into force --- to the exercise of a power or
    the performance of a duty or function, under Part 5 of the other
    Act, as it reads on that day, in relation to a proposal for a
    development, as defined in section 111 of the other Act, that was
    being undertaken immediately before the day on which that section
    206 comes into force, is counted from that day.

- --- 2014, c. 2, s. 252

  - ###### Northwest Territories Waters Regulations {#northwest-territories-waters-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [252]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the Northwest Territories Waters
    Regulations, that are made under the [Northwest Territories Waters
    Act](/eng/acts/N-27.3), chapter 39 of the Statutes of Canada, 1992,
    that are in force immediately before the day on which section 182
    comes into force, are deemed, as of that day, to have been made
    under the other Act and shall remain in force, to the extent that
    they are not inconsistent with the other Act, as it reads on that
    day, until they are repealed or replaced.

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 36

  - ###### Definition of [[other Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} {#definition-of-other-act .MarginalNote}

    - [36]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[other
      Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Mackenzie Valley Resource
      Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2).

    - ###### Ongoing proposals for development {#ongoing-proposals-for-development .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Part 5 of the other Act, as it read immediately
      before the day on which section 22 comes into force, continues to
      apply to a proposal for [[development]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
      defined in subsection 111(1) of the other Act, that, immediately
      before that day, was being considered by any of the following:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a [[designated regulatory
        agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in that subsection
        111(1), for the purposes of sections 131 and 137 of the other
        Act, as those sections 131 and 137 read immediately before that
        day;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [[Tlicho
        Government]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of
        the other Act, for the purposes of sections 131.1 and 137.1 of
        the other Act, as those sections 131.1 and 137.1 read
        immediately before that day;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [[federal Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
        as defined in section 2 of the other Act, and any [[responsible
        minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in that subsection
        111(1);

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Mackenzie Valley Environmental Impact
        Review Board or one of its panels; or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a joint panel established under subsection
        140(2) or paragraph 141(2)(b) or (3)(a) of the other Act or a
        review panel referred to in subsection 41(2) of the [Canadian
        Environmental Assessment Act, 2012](/eng/acts/C-15.21).

## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 1

  - ###### [2014, c. 2, s. 116]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-2-s.-116 .MarginalNote}

    [1]{.lawlabel} Section 7.2 of the [Mackenzie Valley Resource
    Management Act](/eng/acts/M-0.2) is replaced by the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Other requirements to be complied with {#other-requirements-to-be-complied-with .MarginalNote}

      [7.2]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, nothing in this Act, the
      regulations or a licence or permit or development certificate or
      amended development certificate issued under this Act authorizes a
      person to contravene or fail to comply with any other Act or any
      regulation or order made under it, except as provided in that
      other Act, regulation or order.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 13

  - ###### [2014, c. 2, s. 199]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-2-s.-199 .MarginalNote}

    [13]{.lawlabel} Section 111.1 of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Federal Minister --- powers, duties and functions {#federal-minister-powers-duties-and-functions .MarginalNote}

      [111.1]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall exercise the powers
      and perform the duties and functions of any responsible minister
      who is a minister of the Crown in right of Canada for the purposes
      of subsections 130(1) to (3), sections 131.2, 135 and 137.2 and
      subsection 142.21(10).
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 15

  - [15]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 117:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Prohibition --- person or body carrying out development {#prohibition-person-or-body-carrying-out-development .MarginalNote}

      - [117.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The person or body that
        proposes to carry out a development shall not carry it out, in
        whole or in part, unless

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the person or body
          receives a notice under subsection 124(1.1) in respect of the
          development;

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the development is
          exempted from preliminary screening under subsection 124(2);

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the person or body
          receives a copy of a report under subsection 125(6) stating
          that the development will not be a cause of public concern and
          either will not have a significant adverse impact on the
          environment or, in the case of a proposed development that is
          wholly within the boundaries of a local government, is
          unlikely to have a significant adverse impact on air, water or
          renewable resources;

        - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a proposal for a development
          that is the subject of an environmental assessment under
          section 126, the person or body carries it out in accordance
          with the conditions included in a development certificate
          issued under section 131.3, or an amended development
          certificate issued under subsection 142.21(17), with respect
          to that development; or

        - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a proposal for a development
          that is the subject of an environmental impact review under
          section 132, or an examination under section 138, 140 or 141,
          the person or body carries it out in accordance with the
          conditions included in a development certificate issued under
          section 137.4, or an amended development certificate issued
          under subsection 142.21(17), with respect to that development.

      - ###### No contravention {#no-contravention .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person or body may carry out a development,
        in whole or in part, if it is carried out within the period

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} starting on the day on which

          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person or body receives a notice under
            subsection 124(1.1) with respect to the development,

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the development is exempted from
            preliminary screening under subsection 124(2), or

          - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person or body receives a copy of a
            report under subsection 125(6) containing the conclusions
            set out in paragraph (1)(c) with respect to the development;
            and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ending on the day on which that person or
          body receives notice of a referral to an environmental
          assessment under subsection 126(5).

      - ###### Exception {#exception .MarginalNote}

        [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if section 119
        applies.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 16

  - [16]{.lawlabel} Section 124 of the Act is amended by adding the
    following after subsection (1):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Notice of exemption {#notice-of-exemption .MarginalNote}

      [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If the development is exempted from preliminary
      screening for a reason referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or (b), the
      regulatory authority or designated regulatory agency shall so
      notify the person or body that proposes to carry out the
      development in writing.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 18

  - ###### [2005, c. 1, s. 79]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-1-s.-79 .MarginalNote}

    [18]{.lawlabel} Section 129 of the Act is repealed.

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 19

  - ###### [2005, c. 1, s. 80(2); 2014, c. 2, s. 208(4)(F)]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-1-s.-802-2014-c.-2-s.-2084f .MarginalNote}

    [19]{.lawlabel} Subsection 130(5) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Effect of decision {#effect-of-decision .MarginalNote}

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister and responsible ministers
      shall carry out a decision made under this section to the extent
      of their respective authorities. In addition, a first nation,
      local government, regulatory authority or department or agency of
      the federal or territorial government affected by a decision made
      under this section shall act in conformity with the decision ---
      including by implementing the conditions set out in a development
      certificate issued under section 131.3, or an amended development
      certificate issued under subsection 142.21(17), in relation to the
      proposed development --- to the extent of their respective
      authorities.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 20

  - [20]{.lawlabel} Section 131 of the Act is amended by adding the
    following after subsection (1.6):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Provision of decision {#provision-of-decision .MarginalNote}

      [(1.7)]{.lawlabel} The designated regulatory agency shall provide
      a decision made under subsection (1) to the Review Board.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 21

  - [21]{.lawlabel} Section 131.1 of the Act is amended by adding the
    following after subsection (1):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Provision of decision {#provision-of-decision-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government shall provide a decision
      made under subsection (1) to the Review Board.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 19, s. 22

  - [22]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 131.2:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Development certificate {#development-certificate .MarginalNote}

      - [131.3]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Review Board shall issue
        a development certificate to the person or body that proposes to
        carry out the development if

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Review Board has made a determination
          under paragraph 128(1)(a) relating to that development and
          neither an order nor a referral is made under either paragraph
          130(1)(a) or (c) relating to that development within 10 days
          after the day on which the Review Board receives confirmation
          that the federal Minister received its report relating to that
          determination made under subsection 128(2); or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the federal Minister and the responsible
          ministers adopt, under paragraph 130(1)(b), with or without
          modifications, the Review Board's recommendation made under
          subparagraph 128(1)(b)(ii) and neither the designated
          regulatory agency nor the Tlicho Government rejects that
          recommendation under paragraph 131(1)(b) or 131.1(1)(b),
          respectively.

      - ###### Content of certificate {#content-of-certificate .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} A development certificate shall indicate that
        the environmental assessment of the development has been
        completed and that the person or body that proposes to carry out
        the development may carry it out if they comply with the
        conditions set out in the certificate, obtain any licence,
        permit or other authorization required under any Act of
        Parliament or any territorial law or Tlicho law and comply with
        any other requirements set out in such an Act or law.

      - ###### Conditions {#conditions .MarginalNote}

        [(3)]{.lawlabel} A development certificate issued under
        paragraph (1)(b) shall set out the conditions that the person or
        body that proposes to carry out the development is to comply
        with, namely, the implementation of the following measures:

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the federal Minister and the responsible
          ministers agree to adopt, without modifications, a
          recommendation made under subparagraph 128(1)(b)(ii), the
          measures that are to be implemented, in whole or in part, by
          that person or body as specified in the recommendation; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the federal Minister and the responsible
          ministers agree to adopt a recommendation made under
          subparagraph 128(1)(b)(ii) with modifications, the measures
          that are to be implemented, in whole or in part, by that
          person or body as specified by those ministers in their
          decision made under subparagraph 130(1)(b)(ii).

      - ###### Time limit {#time-limit .MarginalNote}

        [(4)]{.lawlabel} A development certificate shall be issued,

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of paragraph (1)(a), within 20
          days after the expiry of the 10-day time limit set out in that
          paragraph; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of paragraph (1)(b), within 30
          days after the first day on which the Review Board has
          received all applicable decisions.

      - ###### Extension of time limit {#extension-of-time-limit .MarginalNote}

        [(5)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister may, at the request of the
        Review Board, extend the time limit referred to in
        subsection (4) by a maximum of 45 days to take into account
        circumstances that are specific to the proposal.

      - ###### Provision of certificate {#provision-of-certificate .MarginalNote}

        [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Review Board shall provide a copy of the
        development certificate to the federal Minister and to every
        first nation, local government, regulatory authority and
        department and agency referred to in subsection 130(4).

      - ###### [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) {#statutory-instruments-act .MarginalNote}

        [(7)]{.lawlabel} Development certificates are not statutory
        instruments for the purposes of the [Statutory Instruments
        Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

    - ###### Duty --- regulatory authorities {#duty-regulatory-authorities .MarginalNote}

      [131.4]{.lawlabel} Each regulatory authority shall, to the extent
      of its authority to do so, incorporate the conditions referred to
      in subsection 131.3(3) into any licence, permit or other
      authorization that it issues, amends or renews.
    :::
    ::::

  - - [23]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 136(1) of the Act is

      ::: AmendedText

        - [136]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The federal Minister shall

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 136(2) of the Act is replaced by the

  - [25]{.lawlabel} Section 137.1 of the Act is amended by adding the

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Provision of decision {#provision-of-decision-3 .MarginalNote}

      [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The Tlicho Government shall provide a decision
